> DEVELOPER

Implement the following plan:

# Nachbarländer auf der Karte + automatische Rufzeichen-Zuordnung

## Context
Nachbarländer nehmen an BOS-ARSA-Übungen teil (z.B. S55UJE aus Slowenien). Die Karte zeigt derzeit nur Österreichs 9 Bundesländer. Ausländische Teilnehmer werden falsch zugeordnet (z.B. S55UJE → "05" OÖ statt "10" Slowenien), weil der Server `SUBSTR(o.callsign, 3, 1)` verwendet, was nur für OE-Rufzeichen funktioniert.

Ziel: Nachbarländer als Hintergrund auf der Karte zeigen, Statistiken erweitern, Rufzeichen automatisch korrekt zuordnen.

## Changes

### 1. Server: Callsign-to-Country Utility (NEW FILE)
**`server/src/utils/callsignCountry.ts`**

```typescript
export function callsignToCountryCode(callsign: string): string | null
```

Mapping (longest prefix first):
- `OE1`-`OE9` → `"01"`-`"09"` (Austrian states)
- `HB0` → `"16"` (Liechtenstein — before HB!)
- `HB` → `"17"` (Schweiz)
- `S5` → `"10"` (Slowenien)
- `OK`/`OL` → `"11"` (Tschechien)
- `D[A-R]` → `"12"` (Deutschland)
- `I` → `"13"` (Italien)
- `OM` → `"14"` (Slowakei)
- `9A` → `"15"` (Kroatien)
- `HA`/`HG` → `"18"` (Ungarn)

Strip `/P`, `/M`, `/OE8` suffixes before matching.

### 2. Server: Migration to backfill bundesland_code
**`server/src/db/schema.ts`** — add after existing migrations

- SELECT all operators WHERE `bundesland_code IS NULL`
- For each, compute code via `callsignToCountryCode()` and UPDATE
- Also used for OE callsigns that were stored with null bundesland_code

### 3. Server: Use utility in operator creation
**`server/src/routes/operators.ts`** — POST handler

- If `bundesland_code` not provided, auto-derive from callsign
- `const derivedBl = bundesland_code || callsignToCountryCode(callsign)`

### 4. Server: Simplify stats SQL
**`server/src/routes/exercises.ts`** — lines 128-155

Remove broken `'0' || SUBSTR(o.callsign, 3, 1)` fallback. After migration, all operators have `bundesland_code`, so queries simplify to:

```sql
-- blStats: just use o.bundesland_code directly
LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
GROUP BY o.bundesland_code

-- participants: remove the bl2 double-join hack
LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
```

### 5. Frontend: Neighbor country SVG paths
**`client/src/components/AustriaMap.tsx`**

- Use the known geo-projection: `svgX = (lon - 9.5009) / 7.6623 * 612.94`, `svgY = (49.0153 - lat) / 2.6440 * 313.55`
- Get GeoJSON from Natural Earth (110m simplified), project to SVG, clip to expanded area
- Hardcode resulting paths as `NEIGHBOR_PATHS` (codes `"10"`-`"18"`)
- Expand viewBox from `"-5 -5 623 324"` to ~`"-100 -100 900 550"` to show neighbor borders
- Render neighbors as background layer: light gray fill (`#e5e7eb`), thin gray stroke, 0.6 opacity
- Countries with data: fill with blue scale (same as Austrian states)
- Change internal key scheme from `"OE1"`-`"OE9"` to `"01"`-`"09"` (DB codes)
- Add `BL_SHORT` map for display labels: `"01"→"OE1"`, `"10"→"SI"`, `"12"→"DE"`, etc.

### 6. Frontend: Fix ReportsPage data mapping
**`client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx`** — line 248

Change from:
```tsx
code: `OE${parseInt(bl.bundesland_code, 10)}`
```
To:
```tsx
code: bl.bundesland_code
```

Update heading: "Teilnehmer nach Bundesland / Land"

## Files Modified
1. `server/src/utils/callsignCountry.ts` (NEW)
2. `server/src/db/schema.ts` (migration)
3. `server/src/routes/operators.ts` (auto-derive bundesland_code)
4. `server/src/routes/exercises.ts` (simplify SQL)
5. `client/src/components/AustriaMap.tsx` (add neighbors, change keys)
6. `client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx` (fix data mapping)

## Verification
1. `nix-shell -p nodejs --run "npm run build"` compiles
2. Server starts, migration backfills existing operators
3. Creating operator with foreign callsign auto-assigns correct country
4. ReportsPage shows neighbor countries on map with correct stats
5. Austrian Bundesländer still display correctly
6. Deploy to production via `bash deploy-production.sh`

     1→import { Router } from 'express';
     2→import { getDb } from '../db/database.js';
     3→
     4→export const operatorsRouter = Router();
     5→
     6→// Search/list operators
     7→operatorsRouter.get('/', (req, res) => {
     8→  const db = getDb();
     9→  const q = req.query.q as string;
    10→  if (q) {
    11→    const pattern = `%${q.toUpperCase()}%`;
    12→    const operators = db.prepare(`
    13→      SELECT o.*, bl.name as bundesland_name,
    14→        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM signal_reports sr WHERE sr.operator_id = o.id AND sr.readability IS NOT NULL) as total_reports
    15→      FROM operators o
    16→      LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
    17→        OR (o.bundesland_code IS NULL AND bl.code = '0' || SUBSTR(o.callsign, 3, 1))
    18→      WHERE o.callsign LIKE ? OR UPPER(o.name) LIKE ? OR UPPER(o.qth) LIKE ?
    19→      ORDER BY o.callsign LIMIT 50
    20→    `).all(pattern, pattern, pattern);
    21→    res.json(operators);
    22→  } else {
    23→    const limit = Math.min(Math.max(parseInt(req.query.limit as string) || 100, 1), 1000);
    24→    const offset = Math.max(parseInt(req.query.offset as string) || 0, 0);
    25→    const operators = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM operators ORDER BY callsign LIMIT ? OFFSET ?').all(limit, offset);
    26→    const total = (db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM operators').get() as any).c;
    27→    res.json({ operators, total });
    28→  }
    29→});
    30→
    31→// Get single operator with history
    32→operatorsRouter.get('/:id', (req, res) => {
    33→  const db = getDb();
    34→  const operator = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM operators WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id);
    35→  if (!operator) {
    36→    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Operator nicht gefunden' });
    37→    return;
    38→  }
    39→  const history = db.prepare(`
    40→    SELECT e.date, e.id as exercise_id,
    41→      GROUP_CONCAT(r.short_name || ': ' ||
    42→        CASE WHEN sr.is_op_marker = 1 THEN 'OP'
    43→        ELSE COALESCE(sr.readability || '/' || sr.strength || COALESCE(sr.db_over_s9, ''), '—')
    44→        END, ' | ') as reports
    45→    FROM signal_reports sr
    46→    JOIN exercises e ON e.id = sr.exercise_id
    47→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
    48→    WHERE sr.operator_id = ?
    49→    GROUP BY e.id
    50→    ORDER BY e.date DESC
    51→  `).all(req.params.id);
    52→  res.json({ ...operator, history });
    53→});
    54→
    55→// Quick-add operator
    56→operatorsRouter.post('/', (req, res) => {
    57→  const db = getDb();
    58→  const { callsign, name, qth, bezirk_code, bundesland_code } = req.body;
    59→  if (!callsign) {
    60→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'Rufzeichen erforderlich' });
    61→    return;
    62→  }
    63→  try {
    64→    const result = db.prepare(`
    65→      INSERT INTO operators (callsign, name, qth, bezirk_code, bundesland_code)
    66→      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    67→    `).run(callsign.toUpperCase(), name || null, qth || null, bezirk_code || null, bundesland_code || null);
    68→    const operator = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM operators WHERE id = ?').get(result.lastInsertRowid);
    69→    res.status(201).json(operator);
    70→  } catch (e: any) {
    71→    if (e.message?.includes('UNIQUE')) {
    72→      res.status(409).json({ error: 'Rufzeichen existiert bereits' });
    73→    } else {
    74→      throw e;
    75→    }
    76→  }
    77→});
    78→
    79→// Delete operator
    80→operatorsRouter.delete('/:id', (req, res) => {
    81→  const db = getDb();
    82→  const operator = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM operators WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id);
    83→  if (!operator) {
    84→    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Operator nicht gefunden' });
    85→    return;
    86→  }
    87→  const refs = (db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE operator_id = ?').get(req.params.id) as any).c;
    88→  if (refs > 0) {
    89→    res.status(409).json({ error: `Operator hat ${refs} Rapporte und kann nicht gelöscht werden` });
    90→    return;
    91→  }
    92→  db.prepare('DELETE FROM exercise_attendance WHERE operator_id = ?').run(req.params.id);
    93→  db.prepare('DELETE FROM operators WHERE id = ?').run(req.params.id);
    94→  res.json({ ok: true });
    95→});
    96→
    97→// Update operator
    98→operatorsRouter.patch('/:id', (req, res) => {
    99→  const db = getDb();
   100→  const fields = ['callsign', 'name', 'qth', 'bezirk_code', 'bundesland_code', 'is_bos_arsa', 'is_arena', 'is_portable', 'is_exercise_support', 'gender', 'email', 'equipment', 'antenna', 'has_emergency_power', 'has_lighthouse_readiness', 'latitude', 'longitude'];
   101→  const updates: string[] = [];
   102→  const params: any[] = [];
   103→  for (const f of fields) {
   104→    if (req.body[f] !== undefined) {
   105→      updates.push(`${f} = ?`);
   106→      params.push(f === 'callsign' ? req.body[f].toUpperCase() : req.body[f]);
   107→    }
   108→  }
   109→  if (updates.length === 0) {
   110→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'Keine Änderungen' });
   111→    return;
   112→  }
   113→  updates.push("updated_at = datetime('now')");
   114→  params.push(req.params.id);
   115→  db.prepare(`UPDATE operators SET ${updates.join(', ')} WHERE id = ?`).run(...params);
   116→  const operator = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM operators WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id);
   117→  res.json(operator);
   118→});

   102→  const totalReports = (db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = ? AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL').get(eid) as any)?.c || 0;
   103→  const perRepeater = db.prepare(`

   106→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
   107→    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0 AND sr.readability IS NOT NULL

   113→  const bezirkStats = db.prepare(`

   115→      bz.code as bezirk_code, bz.name as bezirk_name, bz.is_capital,
   116→      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
   117→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
   118→    FROM signal_reports sr
   119→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
   120→    JOIN bezirke bz ON bz.code = o.bezirk_code

   126→

   131→      COALESCE(o.bundesland_code, '0' || SUBSTR(o.callsign, 3, 1)) as bundesland_code,
   132→      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
   133→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
   134→    FROM signal_reports sr
   135→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
   136→    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
   137→      OR (o.bundesland_code IS NULL AND bl.code = '0' || SUBSTR(o.callsign, 3, 1))

   139→    GROUP BY COALESCE(o.bundesland_code, '0' || SUBSTR(o.callsign, 3, 1))
   140→    ORDER BY bundesland_code
   141→  `).all(eid);
   142→
   143→  // Participant list: all operators with their report counts

   147→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as report_count
   148→    FROM signal_reports sr
   149→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
   150→    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code

    53→  participants: Participant[];
    54→}
    55→
    56→export default function ReportsPage() {
    57→  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
    58→  const [exercise, setExercise] = useState<any>(null);
    59→  const [stats, setStats] = useState<Stats | null>(null);
    60→  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    61→  const [showParticipants, setShowParticipants] = useState(true);
    62→  const barChartRef = useRef<any>(null);
    63→  const pieChartRef = useRef<any>(null);
    64→  const barCardRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    65→  const pieCardRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    66→  const mapCardRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    67→  const pageRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    68→  const [exporting, setExporting] = useState(false);
    69→
    70→  const createExportHeader = () => {
    71→    const header = document.createElement('div');
    72→    header.setAttribute('data-export-inject', 'true');
    73→    header.style.cssText = 'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;padding:16px 24px;background:#1e3a5f;border-radius:8px;margin-bottom:16px;';
    74→    const img = document.createElement('img');
    75→    img.src = '/bosarsa.jpeg';
    76→    img.style.cssText = 'height:36px;border-radius:4px;';
    77→    header.appendChild(img);
    78→    const text = document.createElement('div');
    79→    const title = document.createElement('div');
    80→    title.textContent = 'BOS-ARSA Log';
    81→    title.style.cssText = 'font-size:16px;font-weight:bold;color:#ffffff;letter-spacing:0.5px;';
    82→    text.appendChild(title);
    83→    const sub = document.createElement('div');
    84→    sub.textContent = 'Im Sinne der Sicherheit';
    85→    sub.style.cssText = 'font-size:10px;color:#93c5fd;letter-spacing:1px;';
    86→    text.appendChild(sub);
    87→    header.appendChild(text);
    88→    return header;
    89→  };
    90→
    91→  const createExportFooter = () => {
    92→    const footer = document.createElement('div');
    93→    footer.setAttribute('data-export-inject', 'true');
    94→    footer.style.cssText = 'text-align:center;padding:12px 0;margin-top:16px;border-top:1px solid #e5e7eb;font-size:12px;color:#6b7280;';
    95→    footer.textContent = 'oeradio.at  \u00B7  bos-arsa.at';
    96→    return footer;
    97→  };
    98→
    99→  const downloadChart = async (cardRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>, filename: string) => {
   100→    if (!cardRef.current || !exercise) return;
   101→    setExporting(true);
   102→    const header = createExportHeader();
   103→    const footer = createExportFooter();
   104→    // Wrap the card temporarily
   105→    const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
   106→    wrapper.style.cssText = 'padding:24px;background:#f3f4f6;';
   107→    const parent = cardRef.current.parentNode!;
   108→    const next = cardRef.current.nextSibling;
   109→    parent.insertBefore(wrapper, cardRef.current);
   110→    wrapper.appendChild(header);
   111→    wrapper.appendChild(cardRef.current);
   112→    wrapper.appendChild(footer);
   113→    try {
   114→      const url = await toPng(wrapper, { backgroundColor: '#f3f4f6', pixelRatio: 2 });
   115→      const link = document.createElement('a');
   116→      link.download = filename;
   117→      link.href = url;
   118→      link.click();
   119→    } catch {} finally {
   120→      // Move card back to original position
   121→      if (next) parent.insertBefore(cardRef.current, next); else parent.appendChild(cardRef.current);
   122→      wrapper.remove();
   123→      setExporting(false);
   124→    }
   125→  };
   126→
   127→  const downloadFullPage = async () => {
   128→    if (!pageRef.current || !exercise) return;
   129→    setExporting(true);
   130→    const header = createExportHeader();
   131→    const footer = createExportFooter();
   132→    // Inject header/footer into actual DOM
   133→    pageRef.current.style.padding = '24px';
   134→    pageRef.current.insertBefore(header, pageRef.current.firstChild);
   135→    pageRef.current.appendChild(footer);
   136→    try {
   137→      const url = await toPng(pageRef.current, {
   138→        backgroundColor: '#f3f4f6',
   139→        pixelRatio: 2,
   140→        filter: (node) => {
   141→          if (node instanceof HTMLElement && node.dataset.noExport === 'true') return false;
   142→          return true;
   143→        },
   144→      });
   145→      const link = document.createElement('a');
   146→      link.download = `BOS-ARSA_Auswertung_${exercise.date}.png`;
   147→      link.href = url;
   148→      link.click();
   149→    } catch {} finally {
   150→      header.remove();
   151→      footer.remove();
   152→      if (pageRef.current) pageRef.current.style.padding = '';
   153→      setExporting(false);
   154→    }
   155→  };
   156→
   157→  useEffect(() => {
   158→    if (!id) return;
   159→    Promise.all([
   160→      apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}`),
   161→      apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}/stats`),
   162→    ]).then(([ex, st]) => {
   163→      setExercise(ex);
   164→      setStats(st);
   165→    }).catch(() => {}).finally(() => setLoading(false));
   166→  }, [id]);
   167→
   168→  if (loading) return <p className="text-gray-500">Laden...</p>;
   169→  if (!exercise) return <p className="text-red-500">Nicht gefunden</p>;
   170→
   171→  // Group bezirk stats by Bundesland
   172→  const byBl: Record<string, { name: string; bezirke: BezirkStat[]; totalParticipants: number; totalReports: number }> = {};
   173→  if (stats) {
   174→    for (const bz of stats.bezirkStats) {
   175→      if (!byBl[bz.bundesland_code]) {
   176→        byBl[bz.bundesland_code] = { name: bz.bundesland, bezirke: [], totalParticipants: 0, totalReports: 0 };
   177→      }
   178→      byBl[bz.bundesland_code].bezirke.push(bz);
   179→      byBl[bz.bundesland_code].totalParticipants += bz.participants;
   180→      byBl[bz.bundesland_code].totalReports += bz.reports;
   181→    }
   182→  }
   183→
   184→  const formatDate = (d: string) => {
   185→    const date = new Date(d + 'T00:00:00');
   186→    return date.toLocaleDateString('de-AT', { weekday: 'long', day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' });
   187→  };
   188→
   189→  return (
   190→    <div ref={pageRef} className="space-y-6">
   191→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
   192→        <div className="min-w-0">
   193→          <h1 className="text-base sm:text-xl font-bold text-[#1e3a5f]">Auswertung</h1>
   194→          <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-gray-500 truncate">{exercise.name} — {formatDate(exercise.date)}</p>
   195→        </div>
   196→        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-shrink-0" data-no-export="true">
   197→          <button
   198→            onClick={downloadFullPage}
   199→            disabled={exporting}
   200→            className="bg-[#1e3a5f] hover:bg-[#2a4a7f] text-white px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-xs sm:text-sm disabled:opacity-50"
   201→          >
   202→            {exporting ? 'Exportieren...' : 'Download Auswertung'}
   203→          </button>
   204→          <Link to={`/exercises/${id}`} className="bg-gray-200 hover:bg-gray-300 text-gray-700 px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-xs sm:text-sm">
   205→            Zurück
   206→          </Link>
   207→        </div>
   208→      </div>
   209→
   210→      {stats && (
   211→        <>
   212→          {/* Summary cards */}
   213→          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-2 sm:gap-3">
   214→            <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-2 sm:p-4 text-center">
   215→              <div className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold text-[#1e3a5f]">{stats.totalParticipants}</div>
   216→              <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-gray-500">Teilnehmer</div>
   217→            </div>
   218→            <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-2 sm:p-4 text-center">
   219→              <div className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold text-[#1e3a5f]">{stats.totalReports}</div>
   220→              <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-gray-500">Rapporte</div>
   221→            </div>
   222→            {stats.perRepeater.map((r) => (
   223→              <div key={r.short_name} className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-2 sm:p-4 text-center">
   224→                <div className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-gray-700">{r.count}</div>
   225→                <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 truncate">{r.short_name}</div>
   226→              </div>
   227→            ))}
   228→          </div>
   229→
   230→          {/* Austria Map */}
   231→          {stats.blStats.length > 0 && (
   232→            <div ref={mapCardRef} className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-3 sm:p-4">
   233→              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2 sm:mb-3">
   234→                <h2 className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-[#1e3a5f]">
   235→                  Teilnehmer nach Bundesland
   236→                </h2>
   237→                <button
   238→                  onClick={() => downloadChart(mapCardRef, `BOS-ARSA_Karte_${exercise.date}.png`)}
   239→                  className="text-xs text-gray-400 hover:text-blue-600 flex-shrink-0 ml-2"
   240→                  title="Karte als PNG herunterladen"
   241→                  data-no-export="true"
   242→                >
   243→                  Download
   244→                </button>
   245→              </div>
   246→              <AustriaMap
   247→                data={stats.blStats.map((bl) => ({
   248→                  code: `OE${parseInt(bl.bundesland_code, 10)}`,
   249→                  participants: bl.participants,
   250→                  reports: bl.reports,
   251→                }))}
   252→              />
   253→            </div>
   254→          )}
   255→
   256→          {/* Charts */}
   257→          {(() => {
   258→            // Use bezirkStats if available, otherwise fall back to blStats (Bundesland level)
   259→            const chartData = stats.bezirkStats.length > 0
   260→              ? stats.bezirkStats.map(bz => ({ label: `${bz.bundesland} ${bz.bezirk_code}`, participants: bz.participants, reports: bz.reports }))
   261→              : stats.blStats.map(bl => ({ label: bl.bundesland, participants: bl.participants, reports: bl.reports }));
   262→            const chartLevel = stats.bezirkStats.length > 0 ? 'Bezirk' : 'Bundesland';
   263→
   264→            return chartData.length > 0 ? (
   265→            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
   266→              {/* Bar chart: Stationen + Rapporte */}
   267→              <div ref={barCardRef} className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-3 sm:p-4">
   268→                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2 sm:mb-3">
   269→                  <h2 className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-[#1e3a5f]">
   270→                    BOS-ARSA Übung am {new Date(exercise.date + 'T00:00:00').toLocaleDateString('de-AT')}
   271→                  </h2>
   272→                  <button
   273→                    onClick={() => downloadChart(barCardRef, `BOS-ARSA_Stationen_${exercise.date}.png`)}
   274→                    className="text-xs text-gray-400 hover:text-blue-600 flex-shrink-0 ml-2"
   275→                    title="Grafik als PNG herunterladen"
   276→                    data-no-export="true"
   277→                  >
   278→                    Download
   279→                  </button>
   280→                </div>
   281→                <div className="h-48 sm:h-[350px]">
   282→                  <Bar
   283→                    ref={barChartRef}
   284→                    data={{
   285→                      labels: chartData.map(d => d.label),
   286→                      datasets: [
   287→                        {
   288→                          label: 'Stationen',
   289→                          data: chartData.map(d => d.participants),
   290→                          backgroundColor: '#1e3a5f',
   291→                        },
   292→                        {
   293→                          label: 'Rapporte',
   294→                          data: chartData.map(d => d.reports),
   295→                          backgroundColor: '#c8102e',
   296→                        },
   297→                      ],
   298→                    }}
   299→                    options={{
   300→                      responsive: true,
   301→                      maintainAspectRatio: false,
   302→                      plugins: { legend: { position: 'bottom' } },
   303→                      scales: {
   304→                        x: { ticks: { maxRotation: 90, minRotation: 45, font: { size: 9 } } },
   305→                        y: { beginAtZero: true },
   306→                      },
   307→                    }}
   308→                  />
   309→                </div>
   310→              </div>
   311→
   312→              {/* Pie chart: Rapporte distribution */}
   313→              <div ref={pieCardRef} className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-3 sm:p-4">
   314→                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2 sm:mb-3">
   315→                  <h2 className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-[#1e3a5f]">
   316→                    Rapporte-Verteilung nach {chartLevel}
   317→                  </h2>
   318→                  <button
   319→                    onClick={() => downloadChart(pieCardRef, `BOS-ARSA_Verteilung_${exercise.date}.png`)}
   320→                    className="text-xs text-gray-400 hover:text-blue-600 flex-shrink-0 ml-2"
   321→                    title="Grafik als PNG herunterladen"
   322→                    data-no-export="true"
   323→                  >
   324→                    Download
   325→                  </button>
   326→                </div>
   327→                <div className="h-48 sm:h-[350px] flex items-center justify-center">
   328→                  <Pie
   329→                    ref={pieChartRef}
   330→                    data={{
   331→                      labels: chartData.filter(d => d.reports > 0).map(d => d.label),
   332→                      datasets: [{
   333→                        data: chartData.filter(d => d.reports > 0).map(d => d.reports),
   334→                        backgroundColor: [
   335→                          '#1e3a5f', '#c8102e', '#d97706', '#0d6efd', '#198754',
   336→                          '#6f42c1', '#fd7e14', '#20c997', '#0dcaf0', '#6c757d',
   337→                          '#2c5282', '#e8a317', '#c70039', '#4361ee', '#2d6a4f',
   338→                          '#9b59b6', '#e67e22', '#1abc9c', '#3498db', '#95a5a6',
   339→                        ],
   340→                      }],
   341→                    }}
   342→                    options={{
   343→                      responsive: true,
   344→                      maintainAspectRatio: false,
   345→                      plugins: {
   346→                        legend: { position: 'right', labels: { font: { size: 10 } } },
   347→                      },
   348→                    }}
   349→                  />
   350→                </div>
   351→              </div>
   352→            </div>
   353→          ) : null;
   354→          })()}
   355→
   356→          {/* Bundesland table (when no bezirk data) */}
   357→          {stats.bezirkStats.length === 0 && stats.blStats.length > 0 && (
   358→            <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow overflow-hidden">
   359→              <div className="bg-[#1e3a5f] text-white px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold">
   360→                Teilnehmer nach Bundesland
   361→              </div>
   362→              <table className="w-full text-sm">
   363→                <thead>
   364→                  <tr className="bg-gray-50 border-b text-left text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase">
   365→                    <th className="px-4 py-2">Bundesland</th>
   366→                    <th className="px-4 py-2 text-right w-28">Teilnehmer</th>
   367→                    <th className="px-4 py-2 text-right w-28">Rapporte</th>
   368→                  </tr>
   369→                </thead>
   370→                <tbody>
   371→                  {stats.blStats.map(bl => (
   372→                    <tr key={bl.bundesland_code} className="border-t hover:bg-blue-50">
   373→                      <td className="px-4 py-1.5 font-medium">{bl.bundesland}</td>
   374→                      <td className="px-4 py-1.5 text-right">{bl.participants}</td>
   375→                      <td className="px-4 py-1.5 text-right">{bl.reports}</td>
   376→                    </tr>
   377→                  ))}
   378→                  <tr className="bg-[#1e3a5f] text-white font-bold">
   379→                    <td className="px-4 py-2">Gesamt</td>
   380→                    <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right">{stats.totalParticipants}</td>
   381→                    <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right">{stats.totalReports}</td>
   382→                  </tr>
   383→                </tbody>
   384→              </table>
   385→            </div>
   386→          )}
   387→
   388→          {/* Bezirk/Bundesland table */}
   389→          {stats.bezirkStats.length > 0 && (
   390→            <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow overflow-hidden">
   391→              <div className="bg-[#1e3a5f] text-white px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold">
   392→                Nebenstationen nach Bezirk
   393→              </div>
   394→              <div className="overflow-x-auto">
   395→              <table className="w-full text-xs sm:text-sm min-w-[360px]">
   396→                <thead>
   397→                  <tr className="bg-gray-50 border-b text-left text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase">
   398→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2">Bundesland / Bezirk</th>
   399→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 text-right w-16 sm:w-28">Teiln.</th>
   400→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 text-right w-16 sm:w-28">Rapp.</th>
   401→                  </tr>
   402→                </thead>
   403→                <tbody>
   404→                  {Object.entries(byBl).map(([blCode, bl]) => (
   405→                    <>
   406→                      {/* Bundesland header */}
   407→                      <tr key={`bl-${blCode}`} className="bg-gray-50 font-medium border-t">
   408→                        <td className="px-4 py-1.5">{bl.name}</td>
   409→                        <td className="px-4 py-1.5 text-right font-bold">{bl.totalParticipants}</td>
   410→                        <td className="px-4 py-1.5 text-right font-bold">{bl.totalReports}</td>
   411→                      </tr>
   412→                      {/* Bezirk rows */}
   413→                      {bl.bezirke.map(bz => (
   414→                        <tr key={bz.bezirk_code} className="border-t border-gray-100 hover:bg-blue-50">
   415→                          <td className="px-4 py-1 pl-8">
   416→                            <span className={`inline-block text-xs font-mono px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-white mr-2 ${bz.is_capital ? 'bg-[#dc3545]' : 'bg-[#6c757d]'}`}>
   417→                              {bz.bezirk_code}
   418→                            </span>
   419→                            <span className="text-gray-600">{bz.bezirk_name}</span>
   420→                          </td>
   421→                          <td className="px-4 py-1 text-right">{bz.participants}</td>
   422→                          <td className="px-4 py-1 text-right">{bz.reports}</td>
   423→                        </tr>
   424→                      ))}
   425→                    </>
   426→                  ))}
   427→                  {/* Gesamt */}
   428→                  <tr className="bg-[#1e3a5f] text-white font-bold">
   429→                    <td className="px-4 py-2">Gesamt</td>
   430→                    <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right">{stats.totalParticipants}</td>
   431→                    <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right">{stats.totalReports}</td>
   432→                  </tr>
   433→                </tbody>
   434→              </table>
   435→              </div>
   436→            </div>
   437→          )}
   438→
   439→          {/* Participants list */}
   440→          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow overflow-hidden">
   441→            <div
   442→              onClick={() => setShowParticipants(v => !v)}
   443→              className="bg-[#1e3a5f] text-white px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold cursor-pointer hover:bg-[#2a4a7f] flex items-center justify-between"
   444→            >
   445→              <span>Teilnehmer-Liste ({stats.participants.length})</span>
   446→              <span className="text-xs">{showParticipants ? '▼' : '▶'}</span>
   447→            </div>
   448→            {showParticipants && (
   449→              <div className="overflow-x-auto">
   450→              <table className="w-full text-xs sm:text-sm min-w-[360px]">
   451→                <thead>
   452→                  <tr className="bg-gray-50 border-b text-left text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase">
   453→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 w-8">#</th>
   454→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2">Rufzeichen</th>
   455→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 hidden sm:table-cell">Name</th>
   456→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2">Bezirk</th>
   457→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 hidden md:table-cell">Bundesland</th>
   458→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 text-right w-16 sm:w-24">Rapp.</th>
   459→                  </tr>
   460→                </thead>
   461→                <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
   462→                  {stats.participants.map((p, idx) => (
   463→                    <tr key={p.callsign} className="hover:bg-blue-50">
   464→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-xs text-gray-400">{idx + 1}</td>
   465→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 font-mono font-medium">{p.callsign}</td>
   466→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-gray-600 hidden sm:table-cell">{p.name || ''}</td>
   467→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1">
   468→                        {p.bezirk_code && (
   469→                          <span className="text-xs bg-gray-200 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono">{p.bezirk_code}</span>
   470→                        )}
   471→                      </td>
   472→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-gray-600 text-xs hidden md:table-cell">{p.bundesland_name || ''}</td>
   473→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-right font-mono">{p.report_count}</td>
   474→                    </tr>
   475→                  ))}
   476→                </tbody>
   477→              </table>
   478→              </div>
   479→            )}
   480→          </div>
   481→
   482→          {/* Export buttons */}
   483→          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-4" data-no-export="true">
   484→            <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-[#1e3a5f] mb-3">Export</h2>
   485→            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
   486→              <button
   487→                onClick={downloadFullPage}
   488→                disabled={exporting}
   489→                className="bg-[#1e3a5f] hover:bg-[#2a4a7f] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium disabled:opacity-50"
   490→              >
   491→                {exporting ? 'Exportieren...' : 'PNG — Download Auswertung'}
   492→              </button>
   493→              {exercise.oe_link_enabled !== 0 && (
   494→                <a
   495→                  href={`/api/v1/export/exercises/${id}/bund`}
   496→                  className="bg-red-100 hover:bg-red-200 text-red-800 px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium"
   497→                  target="_blank"
   498→                >
   499→                  TXT — OE-Link
   500→                </a>
   501→              )}
   502→              <a
   503→                href={`/api/v1/export/exercises/${id}/land`}
   504→                className="bg-red-100 hover:bg-red-200 text-red-800 px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium"

    83→
    84→function getColor(value: number, max: number): string {
    85→  if (max === 0) return '#f3f4f6';
    86→  if (value === 0) return '#f3f4f6';
    87→  const t = value / max;
    88→  const r = Math.round(194 + (30 - 194) * t);
    89→  const g = Math.round(213 + (58 - 213) * t);
    90→  const b = Math.round(236 + (95 - 236) * t);
    91→  return `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;
    92→}
    93→
    94→export default function AustriaMap({ data }: Props) {
    95→  const [tooltip, setTooltip] = useState<{
    96→    x: number;
    97→    y: number;
    98→    code: string;
    99→    name: string;
   100→    participants: number;
   101→    reports: number;
   102→  } | null>(null);
   103→
   104→  const dataMap = new Map(data.map((d) => [d.code, d]));
   105→  const maxParticipants = Math.max(...data.map((d) => d.participants), 1);
   106→
   107→  return (
   108→    <div className="relative">
   109→      <svg
   110→        viewBox="-5 -5 623 324"
   111→        className="w-full h-auto"
   112→        role="img"
   113→        aria-label="Karte von Österreich mit Teilnehmern nach Bundesland"
   114→      >
   115→        {Object.entries(BL_PATHS).map(([code, { d, labelX, labelY }]) => {
   116→          const bl = dataMap.get(code);
   117→          const participants = bl?.participants ?? 0;
   118→          const reports = bl?.reports ?? 0;
   119→          const fill = getColor(participants, maxParticipants);
   120→          const textColor = participants / maxParticipants > 0.5 ? '#ffffff' : '#1e3a5f';
   121→
   122→          return (
   123→            <g
   124→              key={code}
   125→              onMouseEnter={(e) => {
   126→                const svg = e.currentTarget.closest('svg');
   127→                if (!svg) return;
   128→                const rect = svg.getBoundingClientRect();
   129→                const pt = svg.createSVGPoint();
   130→                pt.x = labelX;
   131→                pt.y = labelY;
   132→                const ctm = svg.getScreenCTM();
   133→                if (!ctm) return;
   134→                const screenPt = pt.matrixTransform(ctm);
   135→                setTooltip({
   136→                  x: screenPt.x - rect.left,
   137→                  y: screenPt.y - rect.top - 10,
   138→                  code,
   139→                  name: BL_NAMES[code] || code,
   140→                  participants,
   141→                  reports,
   142→                });
   143→              }}
   144→              onMouseLeave={() => setTooltip(null)}
   145→              className="cursor-pointer"
   146→            >
   147→              <path
   148→                d={d}
   149→                fill={fill}
   150→                stroke="#ffffff"
   151→                strokeWidth="2"
   152→                className="transition-opacity hover:opacity-80"
   153→              />
   154→              <text
   155→                x={labelX}
   156→                y={labelY - 5}
   157→                textAnchor="middle"
   158→                fontSize="10"
   159→                fontWeight="bold"
   160→                fill={textColor}
   161→                pointerEvents="none"
   162→              >
   163→                {code}
   164→              </text>
   165→              <text
   166→                x={labelX}
   167→                y={labelY + 7}
   168→                textAnchor="middle"
   169→                fontSize="9"
   170→                fill={textColor}
   171→                pointerEvents="none"
   172→              >
   173→                {participants}
   174→              </text>
   175→            </g>
   176→          );
   177→        })}
   178→      </svg>
   179→
   180→      {/* Tooltip */}
   181→      {tooltip && (
   182→        <div
   183→          className="absolute bg-gray-900 text-white text-xs rounded px-2 py-1.5 pointer-events-none shadow-lg z-10 whitespace-nowrap"
   184→          style={{
   185→            left: tooltip.x,
   186→            top: tooltip.y,
   187→            transform: 'translate(-50%, -100%)',
   188→          }}
   189→        >
   190→          <div className="font-bold">{tooltip.code} — {tooltip.name}</div>
   191→          <div>Teilnehmer: {tooltip.participants}</div>
   192→          <div>Rapporte: {tooltip.reports}</div>
   193→        </div>
   194→      )}
   195→
   196→      {/* Legend */}
   197→      <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mt-2 text-xs text-gray-500">
   198→        <span>0</span>
   199→        <div
   200→          className="h-3 w-32 rounded"
   201→          style={{
   202→            background: 'linear-gradient(to right, #c2d5ec, #1e3a5f)',
   203→          }}
   204→        />
   205→        <span>{maxParticipants}</span>
   206→        <span className="ml-1 text-gray-400">Teilnehmer</span>
   207→      </div>
   208→    </div>
   209→  );
   210→}

{
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/utils/callsignCountry.ts",
  "content": "// Maps amateur radio callsign prefixes to bundesland/country codes used in the DB.\n// Austrian OE1-OE9 → \"01\"-\"09\", neighbor countries → \"10\"-\"18\".\n\nconst PREFIX_MAP: [RegExp, string][] = [\n  [/^OE1/, '01'],\n  [/^OE2/, '02'],\n  [/^OE3/, '03'],\n  [/^OE4/, '04'],\n  [/^OE5/, '05'],\n  [/^OE6/, '06'],\n  [/^OE7/, '07'],\n  [/^OE8/, '08'],\n  [/^OE9/, '09'],\n  [/^HB0/, '16'],  // Liechtenstein (before HB!)\n  [/^HB/,  '17'],  // Schweiz\n  [/^S5/,  '10'],  // Slowenien\n  [/^OK/,  '11'],  // Tschechien\n  [/^OL/,  '11'],  // Tschechien\n  [/^D[A-R]/, '12'], // Deutschland\n  [/^I/,   '13'],  // Italien\n  [/^OM/,  '14'],  // Slowakei\n  [/^9A/,  '15'],  // Kroatien\n  [/^HA/,  '18'],  // Ungarn\n  [/^HG/,  '18'],  // Ungarn\n];\n\n/**\n * Derive the bundesland/country code from a callsign.\n * Strips portable/mobile suffixes (/P, /M, /OE8, etc.) before matching.\n * Returns null if the prefix is not recognized.\n */\nexport function callsignToCountryCode(callsign: string): string | null {\n  // Strip suffixes like /P, /M, /OE8, /QRP etc.\n  const base = callsign.toUpperCase().replace(/\\/.*$/, '');\n  for (const [re, code] of PREFIX_MAP) {\n    if (re.test(base)) return code;\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n"
}
```

  "new_string": "  // Create index after migration ensures column exists\n  db.exec(\"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_repeaters_bundesland ON repeaters(bundesland_code)\");\n\n  // Migration: backfill operators with null bundesland_code using callsign prefix\n  const nullBlOps = db.prepare(\"SELECT id, callsign FROM operators WHERE bundesland_code IS NULL\").all() as { id: number; callsign: string }[];\n  if (nullBlOps.length > 0) {\n    const updateBl = db.prepare(\"UPDATE operators SET bundesland_code = ? WHERE id = ?\");\n    const backfill = db.transaction(() => {\n      for (const op of nullBlOps) {\n        const code = callsignToCountryCode(op.callsign);\n        if (code) updateBl.run(code, op.id);\n      }\n    });\n    backfill();\n    if (nullBlOps.length > 0) console.log(`  Migration: backfilled bundesland_code for ${nullBlOps.length} operators`);\n  }\n}"
}

{
  "description": "Generate neighbor SVG paths",
  "prompt": "I need to generate SVG path data for Austria's neighbor countries using a specific geo-projection. The projection formula is:\n- svgX = (lon - 9.5009) / 7.6623 * 612.94\n- svgY = (49.0153 - lat) / 2.6440 * 313.55\n\nI need paths for: Germany (DE), Czech Republic (CZ), Slovakia (SK), Hungary (HU), Slovenia (SI), Italy (IT), Switzerland (CH), Liechtenstein (LI), Croatia (HR).\n\nUse the Natural Earth 110m simplified GeoJSON. Fetch from this URL:\nhttps://raw.githubusercontent.com/nvkelso/natural-earth-vector/master/geojson/ne_110m_admin_0_countries.geojson\n\nFor each country:\n1. Extract the polygon coordinates\n2. Project each coordinate using the formulas above\n3. Generate SVG path string (M for first point, L for subsequent, Z to close)\n4. Find label position (centroid of projected coordinates)\n\nReturn the results as a TypeScript object literal ready to paste into code, mapping country codes to `{ d: string, labelX: number, labelY: number }`.\n\nThe country ISO codes in the GeoJSON are: DEU, CZE, SVK, HUN, SVN, ITA, CHE, LIE, HRV.\n\nImportant: clip to a reasonable viewport. The current Austria paths fit in viewBox \"0 0 613 314\". I want to expand the view to show neighbors, so paths that go very far out of range can be simplified but should still be included.\n\nRun this as a Node.js script using `nix-shell -p nodejs --run \"node script.js\"`. Write the script to /tmp/gen_neighbors.mjs and run it.",

**Key notes about the results:**

1. **Liechtenstein (LI)** was too small for the 110m dataset, so it was fetched from the 50m Natural Earth dataset. It's a tiny polygon near coordinates (3.7, 222) -- right at the Swiss/Austrian border as expected.

2. **Coordinate ranges relative to Austria's viewBox (0 0 613 314):**
   - **DE** (Germany): extends far north (y down to -705) and west (x down to -281)
   - **CZ** (Czech Republic): north of Austria (y from -249 to 50)
   - **SK** (Slovakia): northeast (x up to 1045, y from -66 to 155)
   - **HU** (Hungary): east (x up to 1057, y 46 to 386)
   - **SI** (Slovenia): south (y 257 to 423)
   - **IT** (Italy): extends far south (y up to 1470 for Sicily/Sardinia)
   - **CH** (Switzerland): west (x from -278 to 75)
   - **HR** (Croatia): southeast (y up to 775)

3. **For your expanded viewBox**, you'll want to consider how much of each neighbor to show. Since Italy extends to y=1470 and Germany to y=-705, you may want to use SVG `clipPath` or simply set the viewBox to show a reasonable area (e.g., `-300 -300 1400 900`) and let the far extremities get clipped naturally.

4. The scripts are at `/tmp/gen_neighbors.mjs` and `/tmp/gen_li.mjs` if you need to re-run or modify them.

    83→
    84→function getColor(value: number, max: number): string {
    85→  if (max === 0) return '#f3f4f6';
    86→  if (value === 0) return '#f3f4f6';
    87→  const t = value / max;
    88→  const r = Math.round(194 + (30 - 194) * t);
    89→  const g = Math.round(213 + (58 - 213) * t);
    90→  const b = Math.round(236 + (95 - 236) * t);
    91→  return `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;
    92→}
    93→
    94→export default function AustriaMap({ data }: Props) {
    95→  const [tooltip, setTooltip] = useState<{
    96→    x: number;
    97→    y: number;
    98→    code: string;
    99→    name: string;
   100→    participants: number;
   101→    reports: number;
   102→  } | null>(null);
   103→
   104→  const dataMap = new Map(data.map((d) => [d.code, d]));
   105→  const maxParticipants = Math.max(...data.map((d) => d.participants), 1);
   106→
   107→  return (
   108→    <div className="relative">
   109→      <svg
   110→        viewBox="-5 -5 623 324"
   111→        className="w-full h-auto"
   112→        role="img"
   113→        aria-label="Karte von Österreich mit Teilnehmern nach Bundesland"
   114→      >
   115→        {Object.entries(BL_PATHS).map(([code, { d, labelX, labelY }]) => {
   116→          const bl = dataMap.get(code);
   117→          const participants = bl?.participants ?? 0;
   118→          const reports = bl?.reports ?? 0;
   119→          const fill = getColor(participants, maxParticipants);
   120→          const textColor = participants / maxParticipants > 0.5 ? '#ffffff' : '#1e3a5f';
   121→
   122→          return (
   123→            <g
   124→              key={code}
   125→              onMouseEnter={(e) => {
   126→                const svg = e.currentTarget.closest('svg');
   127→                if (!svg) return;
   128→                const rect = svg.getBoundingClientRect();
   129→                const pt = svg.createSVGPoint();
   130→                pt.x = labelX;
   131→                pt.y = labelY;
   132→                const ctm = svg.getScreenCTM();
   133→                if (!ctm) return;
   134→                const screenPt = pt.matrixTransform(ctm);
   135→                setTooltip({
   136→                  x: screenPt.x - rect.left,
   137→                  y: screenPt.y - rect.top - 10,
   138→                  code,
   139→                  name: BL_NAMES[code] || code,
   140→                  participants,
   141→                  reports,
   142→                });
   143→              }}
   144→              onMouseLeave={() => setTooltip(null)}
   145→              className="cursor-pointer"
   146→            >
   147→              <path
   148→                d={d}
   149→                fill={fill}
   150→                stroke="#ffffff"
   151→                strokeWidth="2"
   152→                className="transition-opacity hover:opacity-80"
   153→              />
   154→              <text
   155→                x={labelX}
   156→                y={labelY - 5}
   157→                textAnchor="middle"
   158→                fontSize="10"
   159→                fontWeight="bold"
   160→                fill={textColor}
   161→                pointerEvents="none"
   162→              >
   163→                {code}
   164→              </text>
   165→              <text
   166→                x={labelX}
   167→                y={labelY + 7}
   168→                textAnchor="middle"
   169→                fontSize="9"
   170→                fill={textColor}
   171→                pointerEvents="none"
   172→              >
   173→                {participants}
   174→              </text>
   175→            </g>
   176→          );
   177→        })}
   178→      </svg>
   179→
   180→      {/* Tooltip */}
   181→      {tooltip && (
   182→        <div
   183→          className="absolute bg-gray-900 text-white text-xs rounded px-2 py-1.5 pointer-events-none shadow-lg z-10 whitespace-nowrap"
   184→          style={{
   185→            left: tooltip.x,
   186→            top: tooltip.y,
   187→            transform: 'translate(-50%, -100%)',
   188→          }}
   189→        >
   190→          <div className="font-bold">{tooltip.code} — {tooltip.name}</div>
   191→          <div>Teilnehmer: {tooltip.participants}</div>
   192→          <div>Rapporte: {tooltip.reports}</div>
   193→        </div>
   194→      )}
   195→
   196→      {/* Legend */}
   197→      <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mt-2 text-xs text-gray-500">
   198→        <span>0</span>
   199→        <div
   200→          className="h-3 w-32 rounded"
   201→          style={{
   202→            background: 'linear-gradient(to right, #c2d5ec, #1e3a5f)',
   203→          }}
   204→        />
   205→        <span>{maxParticipants}</span>
   206→        <span className="ml-1 text-gray-400">Teilnehmer</span>
   207→      </div>
   208→    </div>
   209→  );
   210→}
   211→

 if (max === 0) return '#f3f4f6';\n  if (value === 0) return '#f3f4f6';\n  const t = value / max;\n  const r = Math.round(194 + (30 - 194) * t);\n  const g = Math.round(213 + (58 - 213) * t);\n  const b = Math.round(236 + (95 - 236) * t);\n  return `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;\n}\n\nexport default function AustriaMap({ data }: Props) {\n  const [tooltip, setTooltip] = useState<{\n    x: number;\n    y: number;\n    code: string;\n    name: string;\n    participants: number;\n    reports: number;\n  } | null>(null);\n\n  const dataMap = new Map(data.map((d) => [d.code, d]));\n  const maxParticipants = Math.max(...data.map((d) => d.participants), 1);\n\n  const showTooltip = (e: React.MouseEvent, code: string, labelX: number, labelY: number, participants: number, reports: number) => {\n    const svg = e.currentTarget.closest('svg');\n    if (!svg) return;\n    const rect = svg.getBoundingClientRect();\n    const pt = svg.createSVGPoint();\n    pt.x = labelX;\n    pt.y = labelY;\n    const ctm = svg.getScreenCTM();\n    if (!ctm) return;\n    const screenPt = pt.matrixTransform(ctm);\n    setTooltip({\n      x: screenPt.x - rect.left,\n      y: screenPt.y - rect.top - 10,\n      code: BL_SHORT[code] || code,\n      name: BL_NAMES[code] || code,\n      participants,\n      reports,\n    });\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"relative\">\n      <svg\n        viewBox=\"-300 -120 1100 560\"\n        className=\"w-full h-auto\"\n        role=\"img\"\n        aria-label=\"Karte von Österreich und Nachbarländer mit Teilnehmern\"\n      >\n        {/* Clip to visible area */}\n        <defs>\n          <clipPath id=\"map-clip\">\n            <rect x=\"-300\" y=\"-120\" width=\"1100\" height=\"560\" />\n          </clipPath>\n        </defs>\n\n        {/* Background: neighbor country borders */}\n        <g clipPath=\"url(#map-clip)\">\n          {Object.entries(NEIGHBOR_PATHS).map(([code, { d, labelX, labelY }]) => {\n            const bl = dataMap.get(code);\n            const participants = bl?.participants ?? 0;\n            const reports = bl?.reports ?? 0;\n            const hasPeople = participants > 0;\n            const fill = hasPeople ? getColor(participants, maxParticipants) : '#e5e7eb';\n\n            return (\n              <g\n                key={code}\n                onMouseEnter={(e) => showTooltip(e, code, labelX, labelY, participants, reports)}\n                onMouseLeave={() => setTooltip(null)}\n                className={hasPeople ? 'cursor-pointer' : ''}\n              >\n                <path\n                  d={d}\n                  fill={fill}\n                  stroke=\"#d1d5db\"\n                  strokeWidth=\"1\"\n                  opacity={hasPeople ? 0.9 : 0.6}\n                  className=\"transition-opacity hover:opacity-80\"\n                />\n                {hasPeople && (\n                  <>\n                    <text\n                      x={labelX}\n                      y={labelY - 5}\n                      textAnchor=\"middle\"\n                      fontSize=\"12\"\n                      fontWeight=\"bold\"\n                      fill=\"#1e3a5f\"\n                      pointerEvents=\"none\"\n                    >\n                      {BL_SHORT[code]}\n                    </text>\n                    <text\n                      x={labelX}\n                      y={labelY + 9}\n                      textAnchor=\"middle\"\n                      fontSize=\"10\"\n                      fill=\"#1e3a5f\"\n                      pointerEvents=\"none\"\n                    >\n                      {participants}\n                    </text>\n                  </>\n                )}\n                {!hasPeople && (\n                  <text\n                    x={labelX}\n                    y={labelY}\n                    textAnchor=\"middle\"\n                    fontSize=\"11\"\n                    fill=\"#9ca3af\"\n                    pointerEvents=\"none\"\n                  >\n                    {BL_SHORT[code]}\n                  </text>\n                )}\n              </g>\n            );\n          })}\n        </g>\n\n        {/* Austrian Bundesländer (foreground, on top of neighbors) */}\n        {Object.entries(BL_PATHS).map(([code, { d, labelX, labelY }]) => {\n          const bl = dataMap.get(code);\n          const participants = bl?.participants ?? 0;\n          const reports = bl?.reports ?? 0;\n          const fill = getColor(participants, maxParticipants);\n          const textColor = participants / maxParticipants > 0.5 ? '#ffffff' : '#1e3a5f';\n\n          return (\n            <g\n              key={code}\n              onMouseEnter={(e) => showTooltip(e, code, labelX, labelY, participants, reports)}\n              onMouseLeave={() => setTooltip(null)}\n              className=\"cursor-pointer\"\n            >\n              <path\n                d={d}\n                fill={fill}\n                stroke=\"#ffffff\"\n                strokeWidth=\"2\"\n                className=\"transition-opacity hover:opacity-80\"\n              />\n              <text\n                x={labelX}\n                y={labelY - 5}\n                textAnchor=\"middle\"\n                fontSize=\"10\"\n                fontWeight=\"bold\"\n                fill={textColor}\n                pointerEvents=\"none\"\n              >\n                {BL_SHORT[code]}\n              </text>\n              <text\n                x={labelX}\n                y={labelY + 7}\n                textAnchor=\"middle\"\n                fontSize=\"9\"\n                fill={textColor}\n                pointerEvents=\"none\"\n              >\n                {participants}\n              </text>\n            </g>\n          );\n        })}\n      </svg>\n\n      {/* Tooltip */}\n      {tooltip && (\n        <div\n          className=\"absolute bg-gray-900 text-white text-xs rounded px-2 py-1.5 pointer-events-none shadow-lg z-10 whitespace-nowrap\"\n          style={{\n            left: tooltip.x,\n            top: tooltip.y,\n            transform: 'translate(-50%, -100%)',\n          }}\n        >\n          <div className=\"font-bold\">{tooltip.code} — {tooltip.name}</div>\n          <div>Teilnehmer: {tooltip.participants}</div>\n          <div>Rapporte: {tooltip.reports}</div>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Legend */}\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mt-2 text-xs text-gray-500\">\n        <span>0</span>\n        <div\n          className=\"h-3 w-32 rounded\"\n          style={{\n            background: 'linear-gradient(to right, #c2d5ec, #1e3a5f)',\n          }}\n        />\n        <span>{maxParticipants}</span>\n        <span className=\"ml-1 text-gray-400\">Teilnehmer</span>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

All tasks complete. Here's a summary of what was implemented:

**1. `server/src/utils/callsignCountry.ts`** (NEW) — Utility to map callsign prefixes (OE1-OE9, S5, OK, DA-DR, I, OM, 9A, HB0, HB, HA, HG) to DB codes ("01"-"18"). Strips `/P`, `/M` suffixes before matching.

**2. `server/src/db/schema.ts`** — Added migration that backfills `bundesland_code` for any operators with `NULL` using `callsignToCountryCode()`.

**3. `server/src/routes/operators.ts`** — POST handler now auto-derives `bundesland_code` from callsign when not provided. Also removed the broken `SUBSTR` fallback from the GET search query.

**4. `server/src/routes/exercises.ts`** — Simplified `blStats` and `participants` SQL queries: removed the double-join hack and `COALESCE(...SUBSTR...)` fallbacks. All operators now have `bundesland_code` set by migration.

**5. `client/src/components/AustriaMap.tsx`** — Major update:
   - Changed key scheme from `"OE1"`-`"OE9"` to DB codes `"01"`-`"09"`
   - Added `BL_SHORT` map for display labels (`"01"→"OE1"`, `"10"→"SI"`, etc.)
   - Added `NEIGHBOR_PATHS` with geo-projected SVG paths for all 9 neighbor countries
   - Expanded viewBox to `"-300 -120 1100 560"` with SVG clipPath
   - Neighbors render as background layer (gray when empty, blue-scale when they have data)
   - Country labels show when participants exist

**6. `client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx`** — Fixed data mapping to pass raw `bundesland_code` instead of `OE${parseInt(...)}`. Updated heading to "Teilnehmer nach Bundesland / Land".

> DEVELOPER

     1→#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2→set -e
     3→
     4→# Colors for output
     5→RED='\033[0;31m'
     6→GREEN='\033[0;32m'
     7→YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
     8→NC='\033[0m' # No Color
     9→
    10→# Load environment variables
    11→if [ -f ".env.production" ]; then
    12→    source .env.production
    13→else
    14→    echo -e "${RED}Error: .env.production file not found${NC}"
    15→    echo "Copy .env.production.example to .env.production and configure it"
    16→    exit 1
    17→fi
    18→
    19→# Validate required variables
    20→REQUIRED_VARS="DEPLOY_HOST REMOTE_DIR CONTAINER_NAME IMAGE_NAME CONTAINER_PORT SITE_URL JWT_SECRET"
    21→for var in $REQUIRED_VARS; do
    22→    if [ -z "${!var}" ]; then
    23→        echo -e "${RED}Error: $var is not set in .env.production${NC}"
    24→        exit 1
    25→    fi
    26→done
    27→
    28→# Check for --rebuild flag
    29→REBUILD_FLAG=""
    30→if [ "$1" == "--rebuild" ]; then
    31→    REBUILD_FLAG="--no-cache"
    32→    echo -e "${YELLOW}Rebuild flag set — will build without cache${NC}"
    33→fi
    34→
    35→echo -e "${GREEN}=== Deploying BOS-ARSA Log to $DEPLOY_HOST ===${NC}"
    36→echo "Host: $DEPLOY_HOST"
    37→echo "Remote dir: $REMOTE_DIR"
    38→echo "Container: $CONTAINER_NAME"
    39→echo "Port: $CONTAINER_PORT"
    40→echo ""
    41→
    42→# Step 1: Sync code to remote host
    43→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 1: Syncing code...${NC}"
    44→GIT_URL=$(git remote get-url origin 2>/dev/null || echo "")
    45→ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "
    46→    if [ -d '$REMOTE_DIR' ]; then
    47→        cd '$REMOTE_DIR' && git pull
    48→    else
    49→        mkdir -p '$(dirname $REMOTE_DIR)'
    50→        git clone '$GIT_URL' '$REMOTE_DIR'
    51→    fi
    52→"
    53→
    54→# Step 2: Copy .env for docker compose
    55→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 2: Copying environment config...${NC}"
    56→ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "cat > '$REMOTE_DIR/.env' << 'ENVEOF'
    57→JWT_SECRET=$JWT_SECRET
    58→ENVEOF"
    59→
    60→# Step 3: Build Docker image on remote
    61→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 3: Building Docker image...${NC}"
    62→ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "
    63→    cd '$REMOTE_DIR'
    64→    docker build $REBUILD_FLAG -t '$IMAGE_NAME' .
    65→"
    66→
    67→# Step 4: Restart container (volume is preserved — data persists)
    68→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 4: Restarting container...${NC}"
    69→ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "
    70→    cd '$REMOTE_DIR'
    71→    docker compose up -d --force-recreate --build
    72→"
    73→
    74→# Step 5: Verify deployment
    75→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 5: Verifying deployment...${NC}"
    76→sleep 3
    77→
    78→# Check container status
    79→CONTAINER_STATUS=$(ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "docker ps --filter 'name=$CONTAINER_NAME' --format '{{.Status}}'")
    80→echo "Container status: $CONTAINER_STATUS"
    81→
    82→# Check local port
    83→LOCAL_PORT=$(echo $CONTAINER_PORT | cut -d: -f1)
    84→if ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "curl -sf -o /dev/null http://localhost:$LOCAL_PORT/api/v1/auth/login"; then
    85→    echo -e "${GREEN}✓ Container is running on port $LOCAL_PORT${NC}"
    86→else
    87→    echo -e "${YELLOW}⚠ Container not yet responding on port $LOCAL_PORT — may still be starting${NC}"
    88→fi
    89→
    90→# Check public URL
    91→if curl -sf -o /dev/null "$SITE_URL" 2>/dev/null; then
    92→    echo -e "${GREEN}✓ Site is accessible at $SITE_URL${NC}"
    93→else
    94→    echo -e "${YELLOW}⚠ Public URL not yet accessible — configure Cloudflare tunnel${NC}"
    95→fi
    96→
    97→echo ""
    98→echo -e "${GREEN}=== Deployment complete ===${NC}"
    99→echo ""
   100→echo "To seed a fresh database:  docker exec $CONTAINER_NAME node dist/server/db/seed.js"

{
  "description": "Generate hi-res neighbor SVG paths",
  "prompt": "I need to generate HIGH RESOLUTION SVG path data for Austria's neighbor countries using Natural Earth **10m** data. The 110m data I used before looked terrible — blocky borders that don't align with the detailed Austrian Bundesland paths.\n\nThe projection formula is:\n- svgX = (lon - 9.5009) / 7.6623 * 612.94\n- svgY = (49.0153 - lat) / 2.6440 * 313.55\n\nI need paths for these countries (with their ISO A3 codes in the GeoJSON):\n- Germany (DEU) → code \"12\"\n- Czech Republic (CZE) → code \"11\"  \n- Slovakia (SVK) → code \"14\"\n- Hungary (HUN) → code \"18\"\n- Slovenia (SVN) → code \"10\"\n- Italy (ITA) → code \"13\"\n- Switzerland (CHE) → code \"17\"\n- Liechtenstein (LIE) → code \"16\"\n- Croatia (HRV) → code \"15\"\n\nSteps:\n1. Fetch 10m countries GeoJSON from: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/nvkelso/natural-earth-vector/master/geojson/ne_10m_admin_0_countries.geojson\n   NOTE: This file is large (~24MB). Use streaming/chunked download.\n   \n2. For each country, extract ALL polygon coordinates (handle both Polygon and MultiPolygon types)\n\n3. Project each coordinate using the formulas above\n\n4. Generate SVG path strings. For MultiPolygon, combine all sub-paths with spaces. Round coordinates to 1 decimal place.\n\n5. Compute label positions (centroid of the largest polygon for MultiPolygon countries)\n\n6. IMPORTANT: For the label positions, clip them to be within a reasonable visible area. The Austria paths fit in roughly 0-613 x 0-314. The expanded viewBox will be about \"-300 -120 1100 560\" so labels should be within that range. For countries that extend far beyond (like Italy going to y=1400 or Germany to y=-700), place the label at a position that's actually visible on the map (near the Austrian border).\n\nOutput the result as a TypeScript `Record<string, { d: string; labelX: number; labelY: number }>` object literal.\n\nWrite a Node.js script to /tmp/gen_neighbors_10m.mjs and run with:\n`nix-shell -p nodejs --run \"node /tmp/gen_neighbors_10m.mjs\"`\n\nThe script should:\n- Download the GeoJSON (may take a while, it's big)\n- Process each country\n- Print the TypeScript object to stdout\n- Also print the approximate SVG path length (character count) for each country so I can see the detail level\n\nFor very large countries (Italy, Germany), the paths may be huge. That's OK — SVG handles it fine and we clip with viewBox anyway. But if a single country path is over 50,000 characters, simplify by skipping every other point in that country's rings (Douglas-Peucker-style point reduction).\n\nMake sure to handle rings properly — the first ring of each polygon is the exterior, subsequent rings are holes (interior). Use standard SVG path winding rules.",
  "subagent_type": "Bash",
  "timeout": 300000
}

All 9 countries are valid and properly structured. Here is a summary of the results:

**Output file:** `/tmp/neighbor_paths_10m_output.ts` (148 KB)

**All 9 countries successfully generated from Natural Earth 10m data:**

| Code | Country | Path Length | Subpaths | Label Position |
|------|---------|-------------|----------|----------------|
| 10 | Slovenia | 6,013 chars | 1 | (424.5, 343.8) |
| 11 | Czech Republic | 11,029 chars | 1 | (465, -85) |
| 12 | Germany | 37,667 chars | 22 | (69.6, -100) |
| 13 | Italy | 39,971 chars | 31 | (212.3, 420) |
| 14 | Slovakia | 6,464 chars | 1 | (770, 36.8) |
| 15 | Croatia | 27,197 chars | 25 | (553.4, 420) |
| 16 | Liechtenstein | 288 chars | 1 | (3.2, 222.6) |
| 17 | Switzerland | 8,798 chars | 1 | (-102.8, 262.8) |
| 18 | Hungary | 9,816 chars | 1 | (770, 219.6) |

Key details:

- **No simplification needed** -- all countries came in under the 50,000 character threshold, so full 10m resolution is preserved.
- **MultiPolygon handling** -- Germany (22 subpaths), Italy (31 subpaths), and Croatia (25 subpaths) all include island polygons properly combined into single SVG paths.
- **Label clamping** -- Labels for countries extending far outside the viewBox were clamped: Germany's centroid at y=-245.7 was clamped to y=-100, Italy's centroid at y=651.3 clamped to y=420, Croatia's centroid at y=457.7 clamped to y=420.
- **Rings handled correctly** -- exterior and interior (hole) rings are both included in the path data with standard SVG winding.

The script is at `/tmp/gen_neighbors_10m.mjs` if you need to re-run it, and the TypeScript output is at `/tmp/neighbor_paths_10m_output.ts` ready to copy into your codebase.

```json
{
  "description": "Simplify neighbor paths for map",
  "prompt": "The 10m Natural Earth paths at /tmp/neighbor_paths_10m_output.ts are 148KB which is too large to embed in a React component. I need to simplify them.\n\nWrite a Node.js script at /tmp/simplify_neighbors.mjs that:\n\n1. Reads the generated 10m GeoJSON data and re-processes it with Douglas-Peucker simplification\n2. Uses the same projection: `svgX = (lon - 9.5009) / 7.6623 * 612.94`, `svgY = (49.0153 - lat) / 2.6440 * 313.55`\n\nKey approach:\n- Fetch the 10m GeoJSON from: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/nvkelso/natural-earth-vector/master/geojson/ne_10m_admin_0_countries.geojson\n- For each country, apply simplification AFTER projecting to SVG coordinates\n- Use Douglas-Peucker with tolerance = 2.0 SVG units (this preserves detail near Austria but simplifies distant coastlines)\n- For rings that are entirely outside the visible area (roughly -350 < x < 850, -200 < y < 500), use a higher tolerance of 5.0\n- Drop tiny island polygons (bounding box < 15 SVG units in both dimensions)\n- Round to 1 decimal place\n\nCountries needed (ISO_A3 → code):\nDEU→\"12\", CZE→\"11\", SVK→\"14\", HUN→\"18\", SVN→\"10\", ITA→\"13\", CHE→\"17\", LIE→\"16\", HRV→\"15\"\n\nLabel positions (manually chosen for best visibility near Austrian border):\n- \"12\" DE: labelX: 70, labelY: -50\n- \"11\" CZ: labelX: 465, labelY: -85  \n- \"14\" SK: labelX: 770, labelY: 37\n- \"18\" HU: labelX: 770, labelY: 220\n- \"10\" SI: labelX: 425, labelY: 345\n- \"13\" IT: labelX: 150, labelY: 380\n- \"17\" CH: labelX: -100, labelY: 263\n- \"16\" LI: labelX: 4, labelY: 222\n- \"15\" HR: labelX: 555, labelY: 420\n\nTarget: total output should be under 40KB for all paths combined.\n\nImplement Douglas-Peucker yourself (it's simple):\n```\nfunction simplify(points, tolerance):\n  if points.length <= 2: return points\n  find point with max perpendicular distance from line(first, last)\n  if maxDist > tolerance:\n    left = simplify(points[0..maxIdx], tolerance)\n    right = simplify(points[maxIdx..end], tolerance)\n    return left.concat(right.slice(1))\n  else:\n    return [first, last]\n```\n\nOutput a TypeScript object literal to /tmp/neighbor_paths_simplified.ts\n\nRun with: `nix-shell -p nodejs --run \"node /tmp/simplify_neighbors.mjs\"`\n\nPrint character count per country and total at the end.",
  "subagent_type": "Bash",
  "timeout": 300000
}

The output is 23.7KB, well under the 40KB target. Here is a summary of what was produced:

**Script**: `/tmp/simplify_neighbors.mjs`
**Output**: `/tmp/neighbor_paths_simplified.ts`

**Results per country:**

| Code | Country | Path chars |
|------|---------|-----------|
| 10   | SVN     | 959       |
| 11   | CZE     | 2,028     |
| 12   | DEU     | 6,154     |
| 13   | ITA     | 5,228     |
| 14   | SVK     | 1,102     |
| 15   | HRV     | 3,709     |
| 16   | LIE     | 83        |
| 17   | CHE     | 1,635     |
| 18   | HUN     | 1,754     |

**Total path characters**: 22,652
**Total output file size**: 23,723 characters (~23.7KB)

This is a significant reduction from the original 148KB file. The approach used:

- **Douglas-Peucker simplification** with tolerance of 2.0 SVG units for visible rings, 5.0 for off-screen rings
- **Tiny island filtering**: polygons with bounding box under 15 SVG units in both dimensions were dropped (removes irrelevant Italian islands, German North Sea islands, etc.)
- **1 decimal place rounding** to further reduce coordinate string length
- Germany (DEU) is the largest at 6,154 chars due to its complex coastline and many borders; Italy (ITA) is next at 5,228 chars due to its peninsula shape with many coastal features
- Liechtenstein (LIE) is just 83 chars as expected for such a tiny country

> AGENT

The **"Aktuell"** label is set purely on the client side in `DashboardPage.tsx:58-64`:

1. `today` = today's date (ISO format `YYYY-MM-DD`)
2. It iterates all exercises with `.reduce()` and finds the one whose `date` has the **smallest absolute time difference** from today
3. That exercise's `id` becomes `nearestId`
4. In the table row (line 277), `isNearest = ex.id === nearestId` — if true, the row gets a blue background (`bg-blue-100`) and the red **"Aktuell"** badge

In short: whichever exercise has the closest date to today (past or future) gets the label. There's no server-side status — it's computed client-side every render.

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/pages/DashboardPage.tsx",
  "old_string": "  // Compute nearest exercise to today\n  const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];\n  const nearestId = exercises.length > 0\n    ? exercises.reduce((closest, e) => {\n        const dCur = Math.abs(new Date(e.date).getTime() - new Date(today).getTime());\n        const dBest = Math.abs(new Date(closest.date).getTime() - new Date(today).getTime());\n        return dCur < dBest ? e : closest;\n      }, exercises[0]).id\n    : null;",
  "new_string": "  // Compute nearest exercise today or in the future\n  const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];\n  const futureExercises = exercises.filter(e => e.date >= today);\n  const nearestId = futureExercises.length > 0\n    ? futureExercises.reduce((closest, e) => {\n        const dCur = new Date(e.date).getTime() - new Date(today).getTime();\n        const dBest = new Date(closest.date).getTime() - new Date(today).getTime();\n        return dCur < dBest ? e : closest;\n      }, futureExercises[0]).id\n    : null;"
}
