```
     1→# BOS-ARSA Log
     2→
     3→**Amateurfunk Notfunkübung — Live-Erfassung, Multi-User, Auswertung**
     4→
     5→A real-time, multi-user web application for BOS-ARSA amateur radio exercises across Austria. Multiple operators simultaneously log signal reports from ~100 ham radio stations on repeaters and direct frequencies. BOS-ARSA Log replaces the shared Excel workbook and standalone HTML prototypes with a single unified tool.
     6→
     7→Part of the [oeradio.at](https://oeradio.at) ecosystem.
     8→
     9→## Tech Stack
    10→
    11→| Layer | Technology |
    12→|-------|-----------|
    13→| Frontend | React 18, TypeScript, Vite, Tailwind CSS, Socket.IO client |
    14→| Backend | Node.js, Express, TypeScript, Socket.IO server |
    15→| Database | SQLite via better-sqlite3 |
    16→| Auth | Username/password with bcrypt + JWT tokens |
    17→| Security | Helmet, rate limiting, CORS restriction, non-root Docker |
    18→| Deployment | Docker, Docker Compose, Cloudflare Tunnel |
    19→
    20→## Architecture
    21→
    22→```
    23→/client          React SPA (Vite)
    24→/server          Express API + WebSocket server
    25→/data            SQLite database (Docker volume-mounted)
    26→```
    27→
    28→- RESTful API for CRUD operations, WebSocket (Socket.IO) for real-time sync between concurrent users
    29→- Server serves the built client in production
    30→- SQLite keeps deployment simple — the data volume is small (~1,000 operators, ~200 reports/week)
    31→
    32→## Key Domain Concepts
    33→
    34→| Concept | Description |
    35→|---------|-------------|
    36→| **Operator (Rufzeichen)** | Ham radio callsign, e.g. OE8YML, S55UJE |
    37→| **Repeater/Umsetzer** | Radio relay station with TX frequency, offset, CTCSS |
    38→| **Linked Repeater** | One logical repeater spanning multiple physical sites (Einstiegspunkte) |
    39→| **Einstiegspunkt** | Physical entry point in a linked repeater network, e.g. Hermannskogel (HK) |
    40→| **Signal Report (Rapport)** | RST format, e.g. "5/9+30 HK" = R5 / S9+30dB via Hermannskogel |
    41→| **Exercise (Übung)** | One session with configured repeaters and collected reports |
    42→| **Bezirk** | Austrian political district, identified by 2-3 letter code |
    43→| **Bundesland** | Austrian state (OE1=Wien through OE9=Vorarlberg) |
    44→
    45→## Features
    46→
    47→### Two Data Entry Modes
    48→
    49→- **Frequenzen (Land)** — Repeater-first entry. Select a repeater, enter reports grouped by Bezirk.
    50→- **OE-Link (Bund)** — Geography-first entry. Bundesland/Bezirk hierarchy with linked repeater selection per district. Only available when OE-Link mode is enabled for the exercise.
    51→
    52→### Exercise Setup
    53→
    54→- Bundesland selection grid (OE1–OE9) with one-click activation of all repeaters per state
    55→- ~80 pre-configured Austrian repeaters from OEVSV data
    56→- Custom repeater and simplex frequency creation
    57→- OE-Link toggle for linked repeater mode
    58→- Per-repeater operator (OP) callsign assignment
    59→
    60→### Live Multi-User
    61→
    62→- Socket.IO real-time sync — reports entered by one user appear instantly for all others
    63→- Running totals bar always visible: participant count, total reports, per-repeater counts
    64→- Optimized for speed: autofocus, enter-to-submit, auto-uppercase, keyboard shortcuts
    65→
    66→### Keyboard Shortcuts
    67→
    68→| Shortcut | Action |
    69→|----------|--------|
    70→| `Alt+1` through `Alt+9` | Insert "5/1" through "5/9" in rapport field |
    71→| `Ctrl+1` through `Ctrl+9` | Insert "OE1" through "OE9" in callsign field |
    72→| `Enter` | Submit report and clear fields |
    73→| `Arrow Up/Down` | Navigate autocomplete suggestions |
    74→| `Escape` | Close autocomplete dropdown |
    75→
    76→### Reports & Export
    77→
    78→- Exercise summary with per-repeater and per-Bezirk breakdown
    79→- Bar chart (stations vs. reports) and pie chart (distribution)
    80→- Participants list with callsign, name, location, report count
    81→- Export formats:
    82→  - **PNG** — Full-page screenshot of the analytics view
    83→  - **TXT OE-Link** — Bundesland-grouped text export
    84→  - **TXT Frequenzen** — Repeater-grouped text export
    85→  - **TXT Kombiniert** — Summary statistics with per-repeater breakdown
    86→
    87→### Operator Registry
    88→
    89→- Searchable operator database (callsign, name, QTH)
    90→- Quick-add during live entry when callsign not found
    91→- Inline editing of operator details
    92→- Paginated browsing (50 per page)
    93→
    94→### Security
    95→
    96→- Helmet security headers (CSP, HSTS, X-Frame-Options)
    97→- Rate limiting on login (10 attempts per 15 minutes)
    98→- CORS restricted to configured origin
    99→- JWT secret fail-fast in production
   100→- Request body size limit (1MB)
   101→- Input length validation
   102→- Docker container runs as non-root user
   103→- Port bound to localhost only (Cloudflare tunnel access)
   104→
   105→### User Roles
   106→
   107→| Capability | Admin | Erfasser |
   108→|-----------|-------|----------|
   109→| Create exercises | ✓ | — |
   110→| Configure repeaters | ✓ | — |
   111→| Change exercise name/date | ✓ | — |
   112→| Enable OE-Link | ✓ | — |
   113→| Enter/edit/delete reports | ✓ | ✓ |
   114→| Manage operators | ✓ | ✓ |
   115→| View analytics & export | ✓ | ✓ |
   116→
   117→## Getting Started
   118→
   119→### Prerequisites
   120→
   121→- Node.js 20+
   122→- npm
   123→
   124→### Development
   125→
   126→```bash
   127→# Install dependencies
   128→npm install
   129→cd server && npm install && cd ..
   130→cd client && npm install && cd ..
   131→
   132→# Seed the database with reference data (idempotent — safe to run multiple times)
   133→npm run db:seed
   134→
   135→# Start both client and server in dev mode
   136→npm run dev
   137→```
   138→
   139→The client runs on `http://localhost:5173` with API proxy to `http://localhost:3000`.
   140→
   141→### Production (Docker)
   142→
   143→```bash
   144→# Build and run
   145→docker compose up -d
   146→
   147→# Seed database (first run only — idempotent, won't overwrite existing data)
   148→docker exec bosarsalog node dist/server/db/seed.js
   149→```
   150→
   151→Required environment variables:
   152→
   153→| Variable | Description |
   154→|----------|-------------|
   155→| `JWT_SECRET` | Random 64+ char secret (required in production) |
   156→| `CORS_ORIGIN` | Allowed origin URL (default: `https://bosarsalog.oeradio.at`) |
   157→| `DATABASE_PATH` | SQLite DB path (default: `/data/bosarsalog.db`) |
   158→
   159→## API
   160→
   161→All endpoints are prefixed with `/api/v1/`. Protected routes require `Authorization: Bearer <token>`.
   162→
   163→### Auth
   164→
   165→```
   166→POST /api/v1/auth/login    { username, password } -> { token, admin }
   167→```
   168→
   169→### Exercises
   170→
   171→```
   172→GET    /api/v1/exercises              List all (with summary stats)
   173→POST   /api/v1/exercises              Create { date, name }
   174→GET    /api/v1/exercises/:id          Full exercise with reports
   175→PATCH  /api/v1/exercises/:id          Update (name, organisator, oe_link)
   176→GET    /api/v1/exercises/:id/stats    Live statistics
   177→GET    /api/v1/exercises/:id/reports  All signal reports
   178→POST   /api/v1/exercises/:id/reports  Create report
   179→PATCH  /api/v1/exercises/:id/reports/:rid  Edit report
   180→DELETE /api/v1/exercises/:id/reports/:rid  Delete report
   181→```
   182→
   183→### Exercise Repeaters
   184→
   185→```
   186→GET    /api/v1/exercises/:id/repeaters
   187→POST   /api/v1/exercises/:id/repeaters      Activate repeater
   188→PATCH  /api/v1/exercises/:id/repeaters/:rid  Update OP assignment
   189→DELETE /api/v1/exercises/:id/repeaters/:rid  Deactivate repeater
   190→```
   191→
   192→### Operators
   193→
   194→```
   195→GET    /api/v1/operators         List/search (?q= for autocomplete)
   196→POST   /api/v1/operators         Quick-add new operator
   197→GET    /api/v1/operators/:id     Full profile with history
   198→PATCH  /api/v1/operators/:id     Update operator
   199→```
   200→
   201→### Reference Data
   202→
   203→```
   204→GET /api/v1/reference/bundeslaender
   205→GET /api/v1/reference/bezirke
   206→GET /api/v1/reference/einstiegspunkte
   207→GET /api/v1/repeaters
   208→GET /api/v1/repeaters/:id/einstiegspunkte
   209→```
   210→
   211→### Export
   212→
   213→```
   214→GET /api/v1/export/exercises/:id/bund      TXT (Bundesland-grouped)
   215→GET /api/v1/export/exercises/:id/land      TXT (Repeater-grouped)
   216→GET /api/v1/export/exercises/:id/combined  Summary report
   217→```
   218→
   219→### WebSocket Events (Socket.IO)
   220→
   221→```
   222→Client -> Server:
   223→  join_exercise      { exercise_id }
   224→  leave_exercise     { exercise_id }
   225→  report_created     { report data }
   226→  report_updated     { report data }
   227→  report_deleted     { report_id }
   228→  attendance_updated { attendance data }
   229→
   230→Server -> Client (broadcast):
   231→  report_created     { report, entered_by }
   232→  report_updated     { report, entered_by }
   233→  report_deleted     { report_id, entered_by }
   234→  attendance_updated { attendance, entered_by }
   235→```
   236→
   237→## Seed Data
   238→
   239→The seed script (`npm run db:seed`) is **idempotent** — it only inserts data if the tables are empty, and never deletes existing data. It populates:
   240→
   241→- **18 Bundesländer** — 9 Austrian states + 9 international entries
   242→- **84 Bezirke** — Austrian political districts with capital flags
   243→- **~80 Repeaters** — Comprehensive OEVSV data across OE1–OE9 plus international and simplex
   244→- **12 Einstiegspunkte** — Entry points for the Magdalensberg linked system
   245→- **2 Users** — Admin and operator accounts
   246→
   247→## Conventions
   248→
   249→- German UI text, English code
   250→- Callsigns always uppercase
   251→- RST format: R/S with optional +dB modifier and location suffix
   252→- All timestamps in Europe/Vienna timezone
   253→- API prefix: `/api/v1/`
   254→- Bezirk badges: Hauptstadt = red, normal = gray
   255→- Bundesland headers with blue badges
   256→
   257→## License
   258→
   259→MIT
   260→

</system-reminder>

   265→              <RoleRow label="Bundesländer aktivieren" admin operator={false} />
   266→              <RoleRow label="Rapporte erfassen" admin operator />
   267→              <RoleRow label="Rapporte bearbeiten/löschen" admin operator />
   268→              <RoleRow label="Operatoren verwalten" admin operator />
   269→              <RoleRow label="Auswertung ansehen" admin operator />
   270→              <RoleRow label="Daten exportieren" admin operator />
   271→            </tbody>
   272→          </table>
   273→        </div>
   274→      </Section>
   275→
   276→      {/* ── Tipps ── */}
   277→      <Section title="9. Tipps für die Praxis">
   278→        <ul className="list-disc ml-5 space-y-2">
   279→          <li>
   280→            <b>Vor der Übung:</b> Admin erstellt die Übung, wählt die Bundesländer und aktiviert die benötigten Umsetzer.
   281→            OP-Rufzeichen pro Umsetzer eintragen.
   282→          </li>
   283→          <li>
   284→            <b>Während der Übung:</b> Alle Erfasser melden sich an und öffnen die Übung.
   285→            Rapporte werden live synchronisiert — jeder sieht sofort, was die anderen eingeben.
   286→          </li>
   287→          <li>
   288→            <b>Schnelle Eingabe:</b> Nutzen Sie Alt+1-9 für schnelle RST-Eingabe und Strg+1-9 für OE-Präfixe.
   289→            Nach Enter springt der Fokus automatisch zum nächsten Rufzeichen-Feld.
   290→          </li>
   291→          <li>
   292→            <b>Unbekannte Rufzeichen:</b> Wenn ein Rufzeichen nicht in der Datenbank ist, wird es beim Speichern
   293→            automatisch als neuer Operator angelegt.
   294→          </li>
   295→          <li>
   296→            <b>Nach der Übung:</b> Auf der Auswertungsseite können Diagramme und Textdateien exportiert werden.
   297→          </li>
   298→        </ul>
   299→      </Section>
   300→
   301→      <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 pt-4 border-t">
   302→        BOS-ARSA Log &mdash; Im Sinne der Sicherheit
   303→      </div>
   304→    </div>
   305→  );
   306→}
   307→
   308→function Section({ title, children }: { title: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
   309→  return (
   310→    <section className="space-y-2">
   311→      <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-[#1e3a5f] border-b border-gray-200 pb-1">{title}</h2>
   312→      <div className="text-sm text-gray-700 space-y-2">{children}</div>
   313→    </section>
   314→  );
   315→}
   316→
   317→function Shortcut({ keys, desc }: { keys: string; desc: string }) {
   318→  return (
   319→    <tr className="border-b border-gray-100">
   320→      <td className="px-3 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs whitespace-nowrap">{keys}</td>
   321→      <td className="px-3 py-1.5">{desc}</td>
   322→    </tr>
   323→  );
   324→}
   325→
   326→function RoleRow({ label, admin, operator }: { label: string; admin: boolean; operator: boolean }) {
   327→  return (
   328→    <tr>
   329→      <td className="px-3 py-1.5">{label}</td>
   330→      <td className="px-3 py-1.5 text-center">{admin ? '✓' : '—'}</td>
   331→      <td className="px-3 py-1.5 text-center">{operator ? '✓' : '—'}</td>
   332→    </tr>
   333→  );
   334→}

     3→import Layout from './components/Layout';

    13→
    14→function ProtectedRoute({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
    15→  const { token } = useAuth();
    16→  if (!token) return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;
    17→  return <>{children}</>;
    18→}
    19→
    20→function PublicLayout() {
    21→  return (
    22→    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col">
    23→      <nav className="bg-[#1e3a5f] text-white px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 flex items-center gap-2 sm:gap-4 shadow-md">
    24→        <Link to="/login" className="hover:text-blue-200 flex-shrink-0">
    25→          <div className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold tracking-wide leading-tight">BOS-ARSA Log</div>
    26→        </Link>
    27→      </nav>
    28→      <main className="p-2 sm:p-4 max-w-[1600px] mx-auto flex-1 w-full">
    29→        <Outlet />
    30→      </main>
    31→      <footer className="bg-gray-100 border-t text-center py-3 text-xs text-gray-500 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 sm:gap-4 flex-wrap px-2">
    32→        <a href="https://oeradio.at" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="hover:text-gray-700">oeradio.at</a>
    33→        <span>·</span>
    34→        <a href="https://bos-arsa.at" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="hover:text-gray-700">bos-arsa.at</a>
    35→        <span>·</span>
    36→        <Link to="/impressum" className="hover:text-gray-700">Impressum</Link>
    37→        <span>·</span>
    38→        <Link to="/datenschutz" className="hover:text-gray-700">Datenschutz</Link>
    39→        <span>·</span>
    40→        <Link to="/hilfe" className="hover:text-gray-700">Hilfe</Link>
    41→      </footer>
    42→    </div>
    43→  );
    44→}
    45→
    46→function FlexLayout() {
    47→  const { token } = useAuth();
    48→  return token ? <Layout /> : <PublicLayout />;
    49→}
    50→
    51→export default function App() {
    52→  return (
    53→    <AuthProvider>
    54→      <Routes>
    55→        <Route path="/login" element={<LoginPage />} />
    56→        <Route
    57→          path="/"
    58→          element={
    59→            <ProtectedRoute>
    60→              <Layout />
    61→            </ProtectedRoute>
    62→          }
    63→        >
    64→          <Route index element={<DashboardPage />} />
    65→          <Route path="exercises/:id" element={<ExercisePage />} />
    66→          <Route path="exercises/:id/setup" element={<ExerciseSetupPage />} />
    67→          <Route path="exercises/:id/reports" element={<ReportsPage />} />
    68→          <Route path="operators" element={<OperatorsPage />} />
    69→        </Route>
    70→        <Route element={<FlexLayout />}>
    71→          <Route path="/impressum" element={<ImpressumPage />} />
    72→          <Route path="/datenschutz" element={<DatenschutzPage />} />
    73→          <Route path="/hilfe" element={<HilfePage />} />
    74→        </Route>
    75→      </Routes>
    76→    </AuthProvider>
    77→  );
    78→}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/README.md",
  "old_string": "### Two Data Entry Modes\n\n- **Frequenzen (Land)** — Repeater-first entry. Select a repeater, enter reports grouped by Bezirk.\n- **OE-Link (Bund)** — Geography-first entry. Bundesland/Bezirk hierarchy with linked repeater selection per district. Only available when OE-Link mode is enabled for the exercise.\n\n### Exercise Setup\n\n- Bundesland selection grid (OE1–OE9) with one-click activation of all repeaters per state\n- ~80 pre-configured Austrian repeaters from OEVSV data\n- Custom repeater and simplex frequency creation\n- OE-Link toggle for linked repeater mode\n- Per-repeater operator (OP) callsign assignment",
  "new_string": "### Two Data Entry Modes\n\n- **Frequenzen (Land)** — Repeater-first entry. Select a repeater, enter reports grouped by Bezirk.\n- **OE-Link (Bund)** — Geography-first entry. Bundesland/Bezirk hierarchy with linked repeater selection per district. Only available when OE-Link mode is enabled for the exercise.\n\n### Exercise Setup\n\n- Bundesland selection grid (OE1–OE9) with one-click activation of all repeaters per state\n- ~80 pre-configured Austrian repeaters from OEVSV data\n- Custom repeater and simplex frequency creation\n- OE-Link toggle for linked repeater mode\n- Per-repeater operator (OP) callsign assignment\n\n### Interactive Austria Map with Neighbor Countries\n\n- Choropleth map of Austria (OE1–OE9) with participant/report density\n- 9 neighbor countries displayed: Germany, Czech Republic, Slovakia, Hungary, Slovenia, Italy, Switzerland, Liechtenstein, Croatia\n- High-resolution borders from Natural Earth 10m data\n- Countries with participating operators are color-coded by activity level\n- Hover tooltips showing participant count and report count per region\n\n### Automatic Callsign-to-Country Assignment\n\n- Foreign callsign prefixes automatically mapped to the correct country (e.g. S55UJE → Slovenia, DL1ABC → Germany)\n- Supported prefixes: OE1–OE9, S5 (Slovenia), OK/OL (Czech Republic), DA-DR (Germany), I (Italy), OM (Slovakia), 9A (Croatia), HB0 (Liechtenstein), HB (Switzerland), HA/HG (Hungary)\n- Strips portable/mobile suffixes (/P, /M, /OE8) before matching\n- Auto-derived on operator creation; existing operators backfilled via migration"
}
```

## Comprehensive Exploration Report: Stats & Reporting System

### 1. ReportsPage (Client: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx`)

**Location:** Lines 1-523

**API Fetch:**
- Makes two parallel API calls on mount (lines 159-165):
  - `GET /api/v1/exercises/{id}` → Full exercise data
  - `GET /api/v1/exercises/{id}/stats` → Aggregated statistics

**Data Structure (Stats interface, lines 47-54):**
```typescript
interface Stats {
  totalParticipants: number;
  totalReports: number;
  perRepeater: RepeaterStat[];
  bezirkStats: BezirkStat[];
  blStats: BlStat[];
  participants: Participant[];
}
```

**Key Interfaces:**
- **BezirkStat** (lines 16-24): Aggregates participants/reports per district with bundesland context
- **BlStat** (lines 40-45): Bundesland-level aggregation (participants, reports)
- **Participant** (lines 26-33): Individual operator with report count and location

**Rendering Breakdown:**
- **Summary cards** (lines 213-228): Total participants, total reports, per-repeater counts
- **Austria Map** (lines 231-254): Visual representation of blStats (Bundesland level only)
- **Bar chart** (lines 265-310): Participants vs. Reports by district/Bundesland
- **Pie chart** (lines 313-351): Reports distribution
- **Bezirk table** (lines 389-437): Hierarchical view grouped by Bundesland, with district breakdowns
- **Participants table** (lines 439-480): Individual operator list with callsign, location, report counts
- **Export buttons** (lines 483-517): Download PNG or text formats

### 2. Exercise Stats API Endpoint (`/api/v1/exercises/{id}/stats`)

**Location:** `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/exercises.ts`, lines 98-156

**Response Structure:**
```typescript
{
  totalParticipants: number,
  totalReports: number,
  perRepeater: { short_name, count }[],
  bezirkStats: { bundesland, bundesland_code, bezirk_code, bezirk_name, is_capital, participants, reports }[],
  blStats: { bundesland, bundesland_code, participants, reports }[],
  participants: { callsign, name, bezirk_code, bundesland_code, bundesland_name, report_count }[]
}
```

**Key SQL Queries:**

1. **Total Participants** (line 101):
   ```sql
   SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) as c 
   FROM signal_reports 
   WHERE exercise_id = ? AND is_op_marker = 0
   ```
   - Counts unique operators who have made reports (excluding operator markers)

2. **Total Reports** (line 102):
   ```sql
   SELECT COUNT(*) as c 
   FROM signal_reports 
   WHERE exercise_id = ? AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL
   ```
   - Counts actual signal reports with readability data

3. **Per-Repeater Breakdown** (lines 103-110):
   ```sql
   SELECT r.short_name, COUNT(*) as count
   FROM signal_reports sr
   JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
   WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0 AND sr.readability IS NOT NULL
   GROUP BY sr.repeater_id
   ORDER BY r.sort_order
   ```

4. **Bezirk Stats** (lines 113-125):
   ```sql
   SELECT bl.name as bundesland, bl.code as bundesland_code, bl.sort_order as bl_sort,
     bz.code as bezirk_code, bz.name as bezirk_name, bz.is_capital,
     COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
     COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
   FROM signal_reports sr
   JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
   JOIN bezirke bz ON bz.code = o.bezirk_code
   JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = bz.bundesland_code
   WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
   GROUP BY bz.code
   ORDER BY bl.sort_order, bz.code
   ```
   - Groups by **Bezirk (district)**, derives Bundesland from bezirk relationship
   - Counts distinct operators and reports with readability

5. **Bundesland Stats** (lines 128-140):
   ```sql
   SELECT
     COALESCE(bl.name, 'Sonstige') as bundesland,
     o.bundesland_code,
     COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
     COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
   FROM signal_reports sr
   JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
   LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
   WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
   GROUP BY o.bundesland_code
   ORDER BY o.bundesland_code
   ```

6. **Participant List** (lines 143-153):
   ```sql
   SELECT o.callsign, o.name, o.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code,
     bl.name as bundesland_name,
     COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as report_count
   FROM signal_reports sr
   JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
   LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
   WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
   GROUP BY sr.operator_id
   ORDER BY bl.sort_order, o.bezirk_code, o.callsign
   ```

### 3. DashboardPage (Client: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/pages/DashboardPage.tsx`)

**Location:** Lines 1-332

**Approach to Aggregation:**
DashboardPage fetches ALL exercises (line 45: `GET /api/v1/exercises`) and aggregates them **client-side** (NOT via a server endpoint).

**Client-Side Aggregation (lines 68-79):**
```typescript
// Quarterly totals
const quarterTotals: Record<number, { participants: number; reports: number }> = {};
let yearParticipants = 0;
let yearReports = 0;

for (const ex of exercises) {
  const q = getQuarter(ex.date);
  if (!quarterTotals[q]) quarterTotals[q] = { participants: 0, reports: 0 };
  quarterTotals[q].participants += ex.participant_count;
  quarterTotals[q].reports += ex.report_count;
  yearParticipants += ex.participant_count;
  yearReports += ex.report_count;
}
```

**Quarter Computation (line 29-32):**
```typescript
function getQuarter(date: string): number {
  const month = new Date(date + 'T00:00:00').getMonth();
  return Math.floor(month / 3) + 1;
}
```

**Rendering:**
- Summary cards (lines 198-216): Total exercises, year participants, year reports, avg participants/exercise
- Trend chart (lines 221-255): Bar chart of participants/reports per exercise in current year
- Exercise table (lines 264-327): Rows with quarter subtotals inserted dynamically (lines 82-107)

**Key Data Structure (Exercise Summary, lines 21-27):**
```typescript
interface ExerciseSummary {
  id: string;
  name: string | null;
  date: string;
  participant_count: number;
  report_count: number;
}
```

### 4. Exercise List API Endpoint (`GET /api/v1/exercises`)

**Location:** `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/exercises.ts`, lines 9-18

**Query:**
```sql
SELECT e.id, e.date, e.name, e.notes, e.oe_link_enabled,
  (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0) as participant_count,
  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL) as report_count
FROM exercises e ORDER BY e.date ASC
```

- Returns lightweight exercise summaries with embedded participant/report counts
- Used by DashboardPage for quarterly/yearly aggregation

### 5. Export Endpoints

Three export formats:

2. **Land Export** (`GET /api/v1/export/exercises/{id}/land`, lines 60-106)

   - Text format sorted by frequency

3. **Combined Export** (`GET /api/v1/export/exercises/{id}/combined`, lines 109-141)
   - Summary statistics + per-repeater counts
   - Simple text format

All generate RST format strings (readability/strength ± dB offset), download as `.txt` files.

All protected routes require authMiddleware (Bearer token).

### 7. API Service

**Location:** `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/services/api.ts`

```typescript
export async function apiFetch(url: string, options?: RequestInit) {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
  const res = await fetch(url, {
    ...options,
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
      ...options?.headers,
    },
  });
  if (res.status === 401) {
    localStorage.removeItem('token');
    localStorage.removeItem('admin');
    window.location.href = '/login';
    throw new Error('Unauthorized');
  }
  if (!res.ok) {
    const err = await res.json().catch(() => ({ error: 'Fehler' }));
    throw new Error(err.error || `HTTP ${res.status}`);
  }
  return res.json();
}
```

- Wrapper around fetch with token injection
- Auto-redirect to login on 401

3. **operators** (lines 20-41):

4. **repeaters** (lines 43-58):

   - `short_name`, `site_name`, `band`
   - `frequency_mhz`, `offset_mhz`, `ctcss_hz`, `burst_hz`
   - `bundesland_code`, `is_custom`

5. **bezirke** (lines 13-18):

6. **bundeslaender** (lines 6-11):

### Summary: Key Data Flow for Special Reports Feature

**For a "special reports" feature showing accumulated stats across a date range:**

1. **Current single-exercise flow:**
   - ReportsPage calls `GET /api/v1/exercises/{id}/stats` → gets fully aggregated stats in one request
   - Stats include bezirk breakdown, Bundesland rollup, per-repeater counts, participant list

2. **For multi-exercise date range:**
   - **Option A (Client-side aggregation):** Fetch all exercises with `GET /api/v1/exercises`, filter by date range, sum up participant_count/report_count (like DashboardPage does for quarterly)
   - **Option B (Server-side aggregation):** Create new endpoint `GET /api/v1/reports/range?start_date=2024-01-01&end_date=2024-03-31` that runs aggregation SQL filtered by date range
   - **Option C (Hybrid):** Fetch exercise list, then call `GET /api/v1/exercises/{id}/stats` for each, aggregate on client

3. **Key challenges:**
   - Bezirk-level aggregation requires joining through operators → bezirke → bundeslaender
   - Blending data across exercises means tracking unique operators per date range
   - Need to handle "Sonstige" (international operators) with null bundesland_code

4. **Existing patterns to follow:**
   - Use `is_op_marker = 0` filter to exclude operator markers from counts
   - Use `readability IS NOT NULL` filter to count actual reports vs. all entries
   - Group by bezirk_code, then roll up by bundesland_code via bezirke table
   - Order results by sort_order columns for consistent display

## Implementation Plan: "Special Reports" (Sonderauswertung) Feature

### 1. Design Decisions

**1a. Where to put the server endpoint**

Use the existing `/server/src/routes/reports.ts` file. It is currently a placeholder (`"Use /api/v1/exercises/:id/reports"`) and already mounted at `/api/v1/reports` in `server/src/index.ts` (line 58). This is the natural home for cross-exercise aggregated stats. The endpoint will be `GET /api/v1/reports/stats?from=YYYY-MM-DD&to=YYYY-MM-DD`.

**1b. How to handle the date range filter in SQL**

All five existing stats queries in `exercises.ts` lines 98-156 filter by `WHERE sr.exercise_id = ?`. The aggregated version will replace this with a JOIN through the `exercises` table and a date range filter:

```sql
WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (
  SELECT id FROM exercises WHERE date >= ? AND date <= ?
)
```

This is a clean approach because:
- The `exercises.date` column is a TEXT in ISO format (`YYYY-MM-DD`), which sorts/compares correctly as strings in SQLite.
- An `IN (subquery)` pattern avoids changing the JOIN structure of each query.
- Alternatively, you could use `JOIN exercises e ON e.id = sr.exercise_id WHERE e.date BETWEEN ? AND ?` -- this is slightly more efficient and preferred for clarity.

**1c. Page URL and navigation placement**

- URL: `/reports` -- a top-level route, parallel to `/operators`.
- Navigation: Add a "Sonderauswertung" (or "Auswertung") link to the nav bar in `Layout.tsx`, alongside Dashboard, Rufzeichen, and Hilfe.
- Dashboard: Add a link/button on the DashboardPage in the summary section or as a card.

**1d. Component reuse vs. duplication**

Extract the shared visualization sections from `ReportsPage.tsx` into reusable components. This avoids duplicating ~300 lines of chart/map/table rendering. The shared components will be:

- `StatsSummaryCards` -- the 2x2/4-column summary grid
- `StatsCharts` -- bar chart + pie chart section
- `StatsBezirkTable` -- the bezirk breakdown table
- `StatsBlTable` -- the bundesland-only table
- `StatsParticipantList` -- the collapsible participant list

Each component takes the `Stats` interface (and optional extras like exercise name/date for titles) as props.

### 2. Files to Create

#### 2a. `client/src/pages/AggregatedReportsPage.tsx` (new)

This is the main new page component. It contains:

- **State**: `dateFrom`, `dateTo`, `stats` (same `Stats` type), `exercises` (list of exercises in range), `loading`.
- **Date range picker**: Two `<input type="date">` fields with labels "Von" and "Bis".
- **Quick-select buttons**: "Q1", "Q2", "Q3", "Q4", "Jahr" (current year), and optionally "Letztes Quartal" (calculates dynamically). Each sets `dateFrom`/`dateTo` and triggers fetch.
- **Header**: Shows the timespan prominently, e.g. "Sonderauswertung: 01.01.2026 -- 31.03.2026" and "X Übungen im Zeitraum".
- **Exercise list**: A collapsible list showing which exercises are included (name, date, participant_count, report_count).
- **Visualizations**: Uses the shared stats components (map, charts, tables, participant list).
- **Export**: A "Download Auswertung" PNG export button (reusing the same `toPng` pattern from `ReportsPage`).

**Data flow**:
```typescript
useEffect(() => {
  if (!dateFrom || !dateTo) return;
  setLoading(true);
  apiFetch(`/api/v1/reports/stats?from=${dateFrom}&to=${dateTo}`)
    .then(data => {
      setStats(data.stats);
      setExercises(data.exercises);
    })
    .finally(() => setLoading(false));
}, [dateFrom, dateTo]);
```

#### 2b. `client/src/types/stats.ts` (new, optional)

Extract the shared TypeScript interfaces currently defined at the top of `ReportsPage.tsx` (lines 16-54) into a shared types file:

```typescript
export interface BezirkStat {
  bundesland: string;
  bundesland_code: string;
  bezirk_code: string;
  bezirk_name: string;
  is_capital: number;
  participants: number;
  reports: number;
}

export interface Participant {
  callsign: string;
  name: string | null;
  bezirk_code: string | null;
  bundesland_code: string | null;
  bundesland_name: string | null;
  report_count: number;
}

export interface RepeaterStat {
  short_name: string;
  count: number;
}

export interface BlStat {
  bundesland: string;
  bundesland_code: string;
  participants: number;
  reports: number;
}

export interface Stats {
  totalParticipants: number;
  totalReports: number;
  perRepeater: RepeaterStat[];
  bezirkStats: BezirkStat[];
  blStats: BlStat[];
  participants: Participant[];
}
```

Both `ReportsPage.tsx` and `AggregatedReportsPage.tsx` will import from this shared file.

### 3. Files to Modify

#### 3a. `server/src/routes/reports.ts`

Replace the placeholder with a real endpoint. The key endpoint:

**`GET /api/v1/reports/stats?from=YYYY-MM-DD&to=YYYY-MM-DD`**

```typescript
import { Router } from 'express';
import { getDb } from '../db/database.js';

export const reportsRouter = Router();

reportsRouter.get('/stats', (req, res) => {
  const { from, to } = req.query;
  if (!from || !to) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: 'from und to Parameter erforderlich (YYYY-MM-DD)' });
    return;
  }

  const db = getDb();

  // Get exercises in range
  const exercises = db.prepare(`
    SELECT e.id, e.date, e.name,
      (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0) as participant_count,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL) as report_count
    FROM exercises e
    WHERE e.date >= ? AND e.date <= ?
    ORDER BY e.date ASC
  `).all(from, to);

  const exerciseIds = exercises.map((e: any) => e.id);

  if (exerciseIds.length === 0) {
    res.json({
      exercises: [],
      stats: {
        totalParticipants: 0,
        totalReports: 0,
        perRepeater: [],
        bezirkStats: [],
        blStats: [],
        participants: [],
      },
    });
    return;
  }

  // Build placeholders for IN clause
  const placeholders = exerciseIds.map(() => '?').join(',');

  const totalParticipants = (db.prepare(
    `SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) as c FROM signal_reports
     WHERE exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND is_op_marker = 0`
  ).get(...exerciseIds) as any)?.c || 0;

  const totalReports = (db.prepare(
    `SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM signal_reports
     WHERE exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL`
  ).get(...exerciseIds) as any)?.c || 0;

  const perRepeater = db.prepare(`
    SELECT r.short_name, COUNT(*) as count
    FROM signal_reports sr
    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0 AND sr.readability IS NOT NULL
    GROUP BY sr.repeater_id
    ORDER BY count DESC
  `).all(...exerciseIds);

  const bezirkStats = db.prepare(`
    SELECT bl.name as bundesland, bl.code as bundesland_code, bl.sort_order as bl_sort,
      bz.code as bezirk_code, bz.name as bezirk_name, bz.is_capital,
      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
    FROM signal_reports sr
    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
    JOIN bezirke bz ON bz.code = o.bezirk_code
    JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = bz.bundesland_code
    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
    GROUP BY bz.code
    ORDER BY bl.sort_order, bz.code
  `).all(...exerciseIds);

  const blStats = db.prepare(`
    SELECT
      COALESCE(bl.name, 'Sonstige') as bundesland,
      o.bundesland_code,
      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
    FROM signal_reports sr
    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
    GROUP BY o.bundesland_code
    ORDER BY o.bundesland_code
  `).all(...exerciseIds);

  const participants = db.prepare(`
    SELECT o.callsign, o.name, o.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code,
      bl.name as bundesland_name,
      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as report_count
    FROM signal_reports sr
    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
    GROUP BY sr.operator_id
    ORDER BY bl.sort_order, o.bezirk_code, o.callsign
  `).all(...exerciseIds);

  res.json({
    exercises,
    stats: { totalParticipants, totalReports, perRepeater, bezirkStats, blStats, participants },
  });
});
```

**Important note on the `IN` clause with `better-sqlite3`**: The `.get(...exerciseIds)` spread syntax works with `better-sqlite3` because it accepts variadic bind parameters. Since exercise IDs are UUIDs (TEXT), this is safe. For a very large number of exercises, this is still fine because exercises are weekly -- even a multi-year range will have at most ~150 IDs.

**A semantic decision for `totalParticipants`**: The single-exercise stats count `COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id)` per exercise. For aggregated stats, the same query naturally de-duplicates across exercises -- an operator who participated in 10 exercises counts as 1 unique participant. This is the correct behavior for a "how many unique operators participated" aggregate. The per-exercise participant counts are still available in the `exercises` array for the "exercises included" list.

#### 3b. `client/src/App.tsx`

Add the new route and import:

```typescript
import AggregatedReportsPage from './pages/AggregatedReportsPage';

// Inside the protected route block, add:
<Route path="reports" element={<AggregatedReportsPage />} />
```

This goes alongside the existing routes (line 64-68 area).

#### 3c. `client/src/components/Layout.tsx`

Add a nav link for "Auswertung" in both the desktop nav (line 24-31 area) and the mobile nav (line 44-48 area):

```tsx
<Link to="/reports" className="text-xs sm:text-sm hover:text-blue-200 hidden sm:inline">
  Auswertung
</Link>
```

And in the mobile bar:
```tsx
<Link to="/reports" className="hover:text-blue-200">Auswertung</Link>
```

#### 3d. `client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx`

Refactor to import the shared `Stats` interface from `client/src/types/stats.ts` and remove the local interface definitions (lines 16-54). The rest of the component stays the same. Optionally, extract chart/table sections into shared components, but this can be deferred to a follow-up if the initial implementation just copies the rendering logic.

#### 3e. `client/src/pages/DashboardPage.tsx` (optional enhancement)

Add a link/button to the aggregated reports page. For example, in the summary cards section (around line 196), add a card or a small link:

```tsx
<Link to="/reports" className="text-blue-700 hover:underline text-xs">
  Sonderauswertung
</Link>
```

Or add it to the quarter subtotal rows so users can click through to see a quarter-aggregated report.

### 4. Component Structure for `AggregatedReportsPage`

```
AggregatedReportsPage
  |-- Header ("Sonderauswertung" title)
  |-- DateRangeSelector
  |     |-- Quick buttons: Q1, Q2, Q3, Q4, Jahr, Eigener Zeitraum
  |     |-- Date inputs (Von / Bis) -- shown when custom range selected or always visible
  |-- Timespan display ("01.01.2026 bis 31.03.2026 -- 12 Übungen")
  |-- StatsSummaryCards (totalParticipants, totalReports, exerciseCount, avg per exercise)
  |-- ExerciseList (collapsible, shows included exercises with their individual counts)
  |-- AustriaMap (reused component, same props)
  |-- Charts (Bar + Pie, same pattern as ReportsPage)
  |-- BezirkTable (same pattern)
  |-- ParticipantList (same pattern)
  |-- Export section (PNG download)
```

### 5. Quick-Select Button Logic

```typescript
const currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();

const quickRanges = {
  Q1: { from: `${currentYear}-01-01`, to: `${currentYear}-03-31` },
  Q2: { from: `${currentYear}-04-01`, to: `${currentYear}-06-30` },
  Q3: { from: `${currentYear}-07-01`, to: `${currentYear}-09-30` },
  Q4: { from: `${currentYear}-10-01`, to: `${currentYear}-12-31` },
  Jahr: { from: `${currentYear}-01-01`, to: `${currentYear}-12-31` },
};
```

The "Jahr" button defaults to the current year. Optionally, a year dropdown could be added if there is data spanning multiple years.

### 6. Implementation Sequence

1. **Create `client/src/types/stats.ts`** -- Extract shared interfaces. This is a zero-risk change.

2. **Modify `server/src/routes/reports.ts`** -- Implement the `GET /stats` endpoint. Test with curl/browser.

3. **Create `client/src/pages/AggregatedReportsPage.tsx`** -- Build the page with date picker, quick-select, and all visualizations. Initially copy the rendering logic from `ReportsPage.tsx`, adapting it for the aggregated context.

4. **Modify `client/src/App.tsx`** -- Add the route.

5. **Modify `client/src/components/Layout.tsx`** -- Add the nav link.

6. **Refactor `client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx`** -- Update to use shared types from `client/src/types/stats.ts`.

7. **(Optional follow-up)** Extract shared chart/table components to reduce duplication between `ReportsPage` and `AggregatedReportsPage`.

### 7. Potential Challenges

- **Performance with large date ranges**: The `IN (...)` clause with many exercise IDs could grow large. However, since exercises happen weekly, even a full year is ~52 exercises. The SQL queries use indexed columns (`exercise_id`, `operator_id`). This should not be a concern.

- **Unique participants across exercises**: A `COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id)` across multiple exercises naturally de-duplicates. The `totalParticipants` number will be the count of unique operators, not the sum of per-exercise participants. This is the correct and expected behavior, but it should be made clear in the UI (e.g., "42 verschiedene Teilnehmer" or similar).

- **`perRepeater` ordering**: The single-exercise stats order by `r.sort_order`. For aggregated stats, the repeater set may span all configured repeaters across many exercises. Ordering by `count DESC` (most reports first) is more meaningful for an aggregate view.

- **Export**: The PNG export logic from `ReportsPage` uses `toPng` from `html-to-image`. The same approach works for the aggregated page. The filename should include the date range, e.g., `BOS-ARSA_Sonderauswertung_2026-01-01_2026-03-31.png`.

- **Empty state**: When no exercises exist in the selected date range, show a clear "Keine Übungen im gewählten Zeitraum" message.

### 8. Key SQL Queries (Summary)

All five queries follow the same transformation pattern. The single-exercise version:

```sql
WHERE sr.exercise_id = ?
```

becomes:

```sql
WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (SELECT id FROM exercises WHERE date >= ? AND date <= ?)
```

Or equivalently with pre-fetched IDs:

```sql
WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (?, ?, ?, ...)  -- bind exerciseIds array
```

The pre-fetched IDs approach is slightly more efficient because the subquery runs once vs. N times, and it avoids the optimizer needing to correlate. Both approaches produce identical results.

---

### Critical Files for Implementation

- `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/reports.ts` - Currently a placeholder; will become the main aggregated stats endpoint with 5 SQL queries adapting the date-range filter pattern.
- `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx` - Reference implementation for all visualization components (map, charts, tables, export); interfaces to extract and rendering patterns to reuse.
- `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/App.tsx` - Route registration; needs new `/reports` route added to the protected route block.
- `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/components/Layout.tsx` - Navigation bar; needs "Auswertung" link added to both desktop and mobile nav sections.
- `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/exercises.ts` - Contains the existing single-exercise `GET /:id/stats` endpoint (lines 98-156) that serves as the SQL query template for all five aggregated queries.
